Conclusion: 通过专家分离和傅里叶路由机制，Mix-MoE能有效解决多语言翻译中参数干扰的挑战，提升翻译性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown great promise in multilingual machine translation (MT), even with limited bilingual supervision. However, fine-tuning LLMs with parallel corpora presents major challenges, namely parameter interference. To address these issues, we propose Mix-MoE, a mixed Mixture-of-Experts framework designed to train LLMs for multilingual MT. Our framework operates in two distinct stages: (1) post-pretraining with MoE on monolingual corpora, and (2) post-pretraining with MoE on parallel corpora. Crucially, we divide the MoE layers into two specialized groups: Language Model Experts (LM Experts) and Machine Translation Experts (MT Experts). LM Experts are designed to capture and retain the monolingual knowledge learned by the pre-trained LLM. MT Experts, on the other hand, are specifically trained to acquire and store bilingual translation knowledge. Furthermore, to facilitate effective interaction between these specialized experts and leverage potential underlying structural patterns in text, we introduce a routing mechanism enhanced by Fourier Transform features derived from model representations. The experimental results demonstrate that Mix-MoE excels in multilingual MT, significantly outperforming existing baselines and showing notable progress in mitigating parameter interference.

</details>


### [39] [The Path Matters: Learning a Token-Commitment Policy for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24697)
*Bohang Sun,Max Zhu,Francesco Caso,Jindong Gu,Junchi Yu,Philip Torr,Pietro Liò,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TraceLock是一个轻量级插件控制器，通过可学习的轨迹状态策略解决扩散大语言模型中的token承诺控制问题，显著提升了质量-步数权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型解码器依赖手工设计的置信度规则或特定block的接受过滤器，缺乏可学习的token承诺决策机制，导致控制问题未被充分解决。

Method: 提出TraceLock控制器，利用未来稳定性作为自监督信号（解码步t的候选token若与最终解码结果一致则标记为稳定），对可变长度轨迹状态进行评分，决定哪些token提案应被提交到部分解码序列中。

Result: 在问答、数学推理和代码生成任务上，TraceLock在质量-步数权衡上优于启发式和学习的基线方法，并且在跨设置部署下表现尤为稳定。

Conclusion: TraceLock的决策不能简化为标量置信度，表明冻结扩散语言模型暴露了一个超越置信度解码的可学习的承诺轨迹空间。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models promise faster generation by refining many token positions in parallel, but this parallelism introduces a hidden control problem: which proposed tokens should be transferred into the partially decoded sequence at each step? We refer to this decision as token commitment. Existing frozen-generator decoders largely rely on hand-designed confidence rules or block-specific acceptance filters. We argue that token commitment can instead be learned as a reusable trace-state policy. We introduce TraceLock, a lightweight plug-in controller that instantiates this policy for a frozen diffusion language model. Since oracle commitment times are unavailable, TraceLock derives self-supervision from future stability: at decoding step t, a proposed token for position i is labeled stable if it matches the final token at position i after the full decoding trace completes. The controller scores variable-length trace states and decides which active token proposals should be committed to the partially decoded sequence. Once trained for a given frozen backbone, the controller can be deployed across local-window widths, generation lengths, and step budgets without retraining or per-setting calibration. Experiments on question answering, mathematical reasoning, and code generation show that TraceLock improves the quality-step tradeoff over heuristic and learned baselines, with particularly stable behavior under cross-setting deployment. Diagnostic analyses show that its decisions are not reducible to scalar confidence, suggesting that frozen diffusion language models expose a learnable space of commitment trajectories beyond confidence-based decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/BobSun98/TraceLock.

</details>


### [40] [TS-Skill: A Benchmark for Evaluating Analytical Skills in Time-Series Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24703)
*Liying Han,Kang Yang,Oliver Wang,Jason Wu,Pengrui Quan,Gaofeng Dong,Ozan Baris Mulayim,Sizhe Ma,Yuyang Yuan,Dezhi Hong,Mario Berges,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-Skill基准和SKEvol框架，用于细粒度评估大语言模型和时间序列语言模型在时间序列问答中的三种核心分析技能：时间尺度选择（SK1）、时间定位（SK2）和跨区间整合（SK3）。实验发现模型在这三项技能上存在显著且不均衡的能力差距，尤其是SK3对非智能体模型持续构成挑战，而技能级评估能揭示被聚合分数掩盖的时间推理缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列问答基准通常按任务类型或高层推理类别组织，难以诊断模型在信号层面的底层能力。本文旨在通过分解三种可组合的分析技能，实现对模型时间推理能力的细粒度诊断。

Method: 提出TS-Skill基准和SKEvol框架，包括：领域感知的时间序列种子生成、技能控制的问题生成、元数据和代码辅助的答案构建、多阶段信号验证、以及人工审核循环。实验评估了十个最新LLM和TSLM。

Result: 模型在SK1-SK3上存在显著且不均衡的能力差距，SK3对非智能体模型持续具有挑战性，而工具增强的智能体在独立SK3任务上表现出选择性优势。

Conclusion: 技能级评估能够揭示被聚合TSQA分数掩盖的时间推理失败，为改进模型的时序推理能力提供了更细致的诊断视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and time-series language models (TSLMs) are increasingly applied to time-series question answering (TSQA). Unlike text-only QA, TSQA requires models to ground answers in temporal signals whose patterns may occur at different scales, specific time locations, or across separated intervals. However, existing benchmarks are typically organized by task types or high-level reasoning categories, making it difficult to diagnose the underlying signal-level capabilities driving model performance. We introduce TS-Skill, a controlled benchmark for evaluating three composable analytical skills in TSQA: temporal scale selection (SK1), temporal localization (SK2), and cross-interval integration (SK3). TS-Skill provides timestamp-aware questions, broad domain coverage, and human-validated QA quality. To construct the benchmark at scale, we develop SKEvol, a skill-guided agentic framework that combines domain-aware time-series seed generation, skill-controlled question generation, metadata- and code-assisted answer construction, multi-phase signal-grounded verification, and human-in-the-loop curation. Experiments on ten state-of-the-art LLMs and TSLMs reveal substantial and uneven capability gaps across SK1-SK3. In particular, SK3 remains consistently challenging for non-agent models, whereas tool-augmented agents show a selective advantage on standalone SK3. These findings demonstrate that skill-level evaluation can uncover temporal reasoning failures that are obscured by aggregate TSQA scores.

</details>


### [41] [The Tokenizer Tax Across 25 European Languages: Domain Invariance, Cross-Lingual Few-Shot Effects, and the Ukrainian Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24718)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文测量了10个基础模型在25种欧洲语言上的分词生育率（每词的token数），发现英语最低（1.2），希腊语/马耳他语最高（~3.1），差异达2.5倍。乌克兰语因预训练数据不足，比其他斯拉夫语言多付15-18%的token成本。生育率排名跨领域稳定（rho>0.97），且高生育率tokenizer会破坏词法边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词生育率（每词token数）对非英语NLP造成了隐性成本，但缺乏系统性的跨语言测量和量化分析。

Method: 在平行文本上，对10种基础模型、25种欧洲语言测量了分词生育率，生成了首个受控的欧洲tokenizer税地图，并进行了子词分析和跨语言少样本评估。

Result: 英语生育率1.2 tokens/word，希腊语/马耳他语约3.1；语言层级顺序为罗曼语族（1.5-1.7）< 日耳曼语族（1.7-1.9）< 斯拉夫语族（2.2-2.5）< 乌拉尔/波罗的语族（2.7-3.0）。乌克兰语比同源斯拉夫语言高15-18%。跨领域相关性rho>0.97。

Conclusion: 非英语语言因分词器设计缺陷承担了显著的计算和经济成本，特别是形态丰富的语言和预训练数据不足的语言。本文公开了所有测量数据。

Abstract: Tokenizer fertility the number of tokens per word imposes a hidden cost on non-English NLP. We measure fertility for ten foundation models across 25 European languages on parallel text, producing the first controlled tokenizer tax map for the continent. The tax spans 2.5x from English (1.2 tokens/word) to Greek/Maltese (~3.1), following a clear hierarchy: Romance (1.5-1.7), Germanic (1.7-1.9), Slavic (2.2-2.5), Uralic/Baltic (2.7-3.0). Ukrainian (2.7) pays 15-18% more than cognate Slavic languages, reflecting underrepresentation in pre-training data. Fertility rankings are domain-invariant across three text registers (rho > 0.97). A subword analysis reveals that high-fertility tokenizers fragment morphological boundaries rather than preserving them. Cross-lingual few-shot evaluation on four Slavic languages shows that few-shot effects are model-intrinsic, not language-dependent. We release all measurements as a public dataset.

</details>


### [42] [World-State Transformations for Neuro-symbolic Interactive Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24719)
*Santiago Góngora,Luis Chiruzzo,Gonzalo Méndez,Pablo Gervás*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索了一种神经符号架构，结合LLM（Llama 3 70B和Gemini 1.5 Flash）与规则驱动的世界状态转换，以解决纯LLM交互式叙事系统中的故事连贯性问题，实验表明该方法能在保持世界状态一致性的同时促进玩家创造性表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯LLM驱动的交互式叙事系统存在故事连贯性问题，亟需一种既能保持世界状态一致性又不限制玩家表达自由的解决方案。

Method: 基于神经符号架构，利用LLM预测规则系统中的状态转换，触发预编程的世界状态变化。使用Llama 3 70B（开源）和Gemini 1.5 Flash（闭源）两种模型，在英语和西班牙语中进行测试，8名参与者体验两个精心设计的场景。

Result: 转换机制在保持世界状态一致性的同时，鼓励玩家通过文字输入进行创造性互动。

Conclusion: LLM与规则驱动的状态转换相结合，是一种有前景的交互式叙事方法，可兼顾连贯性与玩家表达自由。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have changed the possibilities of Interactive Storytelling systems that process free-text user input. However, as more of these systems are built, evidence continues to mount regarding the story coherence problems that arise when relying solely on them. Recent research suggests that LLMs can effectively predict state changes within rule-based Interactive Storytelling systems, triggering pre-programmed world-state transformations.
  In this paper, we conduct an exploratory evaluation of whether such transformations can serve as a catalyst for player expression while aiming to address the incoherence issues typical of purely LLM-based approaches. Building upon a neuro-symbolic architecture, we conducted experiments using an open-source model (Llama 3 70B) and a closed-source model (Gemini 1.5 Flash), with testing conducted in both English and Spanish. Eight participants played two scenarios, carefully designed to assess different evaluation objectives. Our observations suggest that transformations offer a way to maintain world-state consistency while encouraging players to interact creatively through their written inputs.

</details>


### [43] [ROC Analysis for Evaluating Translation Quality Estimation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24721)
*Evelyn Y. Garland,Carola F. Berger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用ROC分析作为评价自动翻译质量估计系统性能的实用方法，该方法不仅与现有方法结果一致，还提供了支持业务决策的可操作性能洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动翻译质量估计系统的日益普及需要一种实用、面向决策的性能评估方法。

Method: 采用受试者工作特征（ROC）分析来评估翻译质量估计系统的性能。

Result: ROC分析产生的结果与当前主流方法一致，同时提供了额外的优势。

Conclusion: ROC分析是一种有用的评估方法，能提供支持业务决策的可操作性性能洞察。

Abstract: The increasing use of automated translation quality estimation (QE) systems calls for practical, decision-oriented methods for evaluating their performance. We propose that Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) analysis is a useful approach for this purpose. Our study shows that ROC analysis not only produces results consistent with currently prevalent methods, but also offers several important advantages, including actionable performance insights that support business decision-making.

</details>


### [44] [StepGap: A Hybrid NLI-LLM Checker for Step-Level Evidence-Gap Detectionin Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24733)
*Yuelyu Ji,Zhuochun Li,Hui Ji,Daqing He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StepGap是一种混合NLI-LLM决策树，用于检测多跳问答中的步骤级证据间隙，通过三类标签（矛盾主张、无关证据、缺失桥梁）定位问题，在步骤级F1上达到72.0，并揭示了“竞争误差抵消”和“Q-F1陷阱”现象。作为GRPO过程奖励，它提升了模型Exact Match性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答中的错误来源复杂，现有方法缺乏对步骤级证据间隙的细粒度检测与可溯因的修复指引，且存在评估指标（如问题级F1）掩盖真实错误的“陷阱”。

Method: 构建混合NLI-LLM决策树StepGap，输出三类标签（矛盾主张CC、无关证据IE、缺失桥梁MB），每个标签对应具体修复动作。在82个多跳问题（181个标注步骤，κ=0.704）上评估，步骤级F1达72.0。通过消融实验揭示LLM基线存在“竞争误差抵消”，并指出“Q-F1陷阱”。

Result: StepGap步骤级F1=72.0，在LLM基线置信区间内（70.1），但结构更可分解：每层移除均降低F1，而LLM基线四分之三的移除反而提升F1。作为GRPO过程奖励，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct Exact Match从32.1±0.3提升至35.4±0.9，单次运行比Search-R1 GRPO高+5.6 Avg EM。

Conclusion: StepGap提供了比LLM-only基线更可解释、更可靠的错误检测结构，暴露了评估中的“Q-F1陷阱”和基线中的“竞争误差抵消”现象，且能有效用作过程奖励以提升下游推理性能。

Abstract: We present \textbf{StepGap}, a hybrid NLI-LLM decision tree that detects step-level evidence gaps in multi-hop QA and emits one of three typed labels: \textsc{Contradicted Claim} (CC), \textsc{Irrelevant Evidence} (IE), or \textsc{Missing Bridge} (MB), each tied to a concrete repair action. On 82 multi-hop questions (181 annotated steps, $κ{=}0.704$), StepGap reaches sF1$=$72.0, within the bootstrap confidence interval of an LLM-only baseline (70.1) but with a more decomposable structure: every StepGap stage \emph{hurts} F1 when removed, while three of four LLM-only removals \emph{improve} F1 -- a sign of \emph{competing-error cancellation}, where internal stages mask each other's errors. We further expose a \emph{Q-F1 trap}: question-level F1 is mechanically inflated by checkers that flag every step, making step-level F1 the necessary diagnostic. Used as a typed GRPO process reward, StepGap improves Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct Exact Match from $32.1{\pm}0.3$ to $35.4{\pm}0.9$ across three seeds, with the single-run comparison showing a $+5.6$ Avg EM gain over the matched Search-R1 GRPO reproduction.

</details>


### [45] [Who judges the judges? Governance from metrics: a runtime framework for continuous LLM compliance monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24737)
*Jehanne Dussert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于指标的治理"原则，将AI合规从静态审计转变为运行时持续度量信号，并开发了开源框架govllm，通过多个小型法律专家模型（监管法官）进行路由决策，利用评委间的分歧作为监管不确定性信号，以更好地满足欧盟AI法案对持续人工监督的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI合规方法将合规性视为二元的、审计时刻的判定，而非生产系统中连续可度量的属性。这种"合规虚构"在结构上无法满足欧盟AI法案对持续人工监督和部署系统行为漂移检测的要求。

Method: 提出"基于指标的治理"原则，构建govllm框架实现治理驱动的路由架构。核心是监管法官面板——多个按标准（EU AI Act、GDPR、ANSSI、可访问性）专门化的小型语言模型（1.7B-7B参数），将评委间分歧重新定义为监管不确定性信号，触发人工仲裁。验证使用49个标注提示/响应对的地面真实语料库。

Result: 四个小型语言模型的一致率从51.5%（mistral:7b）到69.1%（phi4-mini）不等，没有单一模型在所有标准上占优。发现了三个结构故障模式和特定于法官的位置偏差，该偏差在三种问题顺序条件下将一致率降低多达25个百分点。

Conclusion: govllm作为开源软件发布，支持可复现的AI治理研究。Profile-as-jury设计在经验上得到验证，多个小型专业模型优于单一模型，且评委分歧可作为有意义的监管不确定性信号。

Abstract: Current approaches to AI compliance treat conformity as a binary, audit-time verdict rather than a continuous, measurable property of production systems. We argue that this compliance fiction is structurally ill-suited to the requirements of the EU AI Act, which demands ongoing human oversight and the detection of emergent behavioural drift in deployed systems. We introduce governance from metrics, a principle whereby regulatory compliance is derived as a continuous signal from runtime observability rather than from static assessments. Building on this principle, we present govllm, an open-source framework implementing a governance-driven routing architecture in which model selection is determined by accumulated compliance scores rather than by latency or cost alone. Central to our approach is a panel of regulatory judges - LLM evaluators specialised per criterion (EU AI Act, GDPR, ANSSI, accessibility) - whose inter-judge disagreement we reframe not as noise but as a regulatory uncertainty signal warranting human arbitration. We validate this approach through a ground truth corpus of 49 annotated prompt/response pairs across five regulatory criteria, evaluated by four small language models (SLMs, 1.7B-7B parameters) running fully on-premise. Agreement rates range from 51.5% (mistral:7b) to 69.1% (phi4-mini), with no single model dominating across all criteria - empirically motivating the Profile-as-jury design. We further document three structural failure modes in small regulatory judges and a judge-specific position bias that degrades agreement by up to 25 percentage points across three question-order conditions (original, reversed, permuted). govllm is released as open-source software to support reproducible AI governance research.

</details>


### [46] [Beyond the Target: From Imitation to Collaboration in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24793)
*Jinze Li,Yixing Xu,Guanchen Li,Jinfeng Xu,Shuo Yang,Yang Zhang,Xuanwu Yin,Dong Li,Edith C. H. Ngai,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoSpec是一种协作式推测解码方法，通过强化学习训练仲裁策略，在保留加速优势的同时，在分歧处选择更优的草稿或目标模型输出，超越传统仅依赖目标模型的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推测解码默认目标模型在每个位置都是更优选择，但实验发现草稿模型在部分分歧情况下能给出正确答案，因此需要更灵活的协作机制。

Method: 使用强化学习训练仲裁策略，在草稿与目标模型预测不一致时，智能决定采纳哪一方的输出，实现协作式解码。

Result: CoSpec在保持显著加速效果的同时，在性能上超越了仅使用目标模型的方法。

Conclusion: 推测解码应从"模仿"转向"协作"，CoSpec为这一新视角提供了有效验证。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SPD) accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by letting a smaller draft model propose multiple future tokens that are verified in parallel by a larger target model. The dominant SPD paradigm treats the target model as the sole reliable teacher, accepting a draft token only when it exactly matches the target prediction. This design implicitly assumes that the target is always the better choice at every position. In practice, this assumption does not hold. Although the draft is the weaker model overall, it is not uniformly inferior at the token level. In a meaningful fraction of cases where draft and target disagree, the draft's choice is the one that leads to the correct final answer. Inspired by this, we introduce \textbf{Collaborative Speculative Decoding (CoSpec)}, a generalization of SPD that no longer treats the target model as the sole token-level authority. CoSpec trains an arbitration policy via reinforcement learning to decide whether to accept tokens from the draft or target model, selectively accepting draft tokens at mismatches when doing so is likely to yield a correct final answer. Experimental results show that CoSpec maintains substantial speedups while surpassing target-only performance. By shifting the emphasis from imitation to collaboration, CoSpec suggests a new perspective on speculative decoding.

</details>


### [47] [Repeated Sequences Reveal Gaps between Large Language Models and Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24850)
*Kumiko Tanaka-Ishii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出基于重复子序列的评估框架，通过分析文本中重复子序列的分布及其与Rényi熵的关系，发现自然语言与GPT生成文本在长程结构组织上存在系统性差异，且GPT文本的熵增长模式随模型规模呈现统计显著偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估方法主要基于任务表现或短上下文行为，无法揭示文本生成中的长程统计组织，需要一种能探测文本长程结构重复使用模式的评估方法。

Method: 分析文本中重复子序列的多尺度分布，将其与高阶Rényi熵关联，对比人类撰写文本与长度匹配的GPT生成文本的熵增长模式。

Result: 幂律模型仅能描述有限块长度范围；对数-幂形式对熵增长拟合更优；自然语言熵增长模式稳定一致，而GPT生成文本的指数随模型规模发生系统性显著偏移。

Conclusion: 重复子序列熵可作为定量结构诊断工具，揭示自然语言与LLM输出在长程组织上的系统性差异，超越表面流畅性的评估能力。

Abstract: Evaluating whether large language models (LLMs) capture the structure of natural language beyond local fluency remains an open challenge. Existing evaluation methods, largely based on task performance or short-context behavior, provide limited insight into the long-range statistical organization of generated text.
  We propose a complementary evaluation framework based on repeated subsequences. By analyzing their distribution across scales and relating it to higher-order Rényi entropies, we probe how texts reuse previously established structure under finite-length conditions. Experiments on human-written texts and length-matched GPT-generated texts show that, while power-law models can describe restricted ranges of block length, the observed entropy growth is often equally or better characterized by logarithmic--power forms.
  Across datasets, natural language exhibits stable entropy-growth patterns over accessible ranges, with consistent average behavior despite variability across individual texts. In contrast, GPT-generated texts show systematic and statistically significant shifts in estimated exponents with model size. These results demonstrate that repeated-subsequence entropy provides a quantitative structural diagnostic that reveals systematic differences in long-range organization, distinguishing natural language from state-of-the-art LLM outputs beyond surface-level fluency.

</details>


### [48] [Towards a Universal Causal Reasoner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24873)
*Qirun Dai,Xiao Liu,Jiawei Zhang,Dylan Zhang,Hao Peng,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UniCo框架，通过覆盖18种因果查询类型并将符号因果示例转化为代码与自然语言形式，生成高质量训练数据。基于66.6K实例微调后，模型在因果推理任务上平均提升22.9%，在分布外基准上提升8.1%，并在真实场景中展现出更忠实的推理痕迹，表明因果训练能赋予LLM因果思维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推理数据工作多聚焦于特定方面的基准测试，缺乏用于训练具备泛化能力的因果推理器的数据资源。

Method: 提出UniCo数据生成框架，涵盖Pearl因果阶梯上的18种查询类型，将符号示例转化为代码和自然语言形式，并通过精确因果推理验证答案、过滤推理捷径。

Result: 在66.6K实例上微调后，Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Instruct在18种分布内查询上平均提升22.9%；在7个分布外因果基准上比最先进方法提升8.1%；在真实医疗、法律和表格推理任务中，忠实性指标平均提升20.2%。

Conclusion: 以因果为中心的训练不仅能增强因果推理能力，还能赋予LLM在通用推理任务中的因果思维，产生更忠实的推理轨迹。

Abstract: Despite the importance of causal reasoning, training LLMs to reason causally remains underexplored. Existing data efforts mostly focus on benchmarking LLMs on specific aspects of causality, making them less suitable for training generalizable causal reasoners. To address this, we propose UniCo, a data generation framework that both (1) addresses 18 causal query types across Pearl's Causal Ladder and (2) translates natively symbolic examples into code and natural language forms to simulate real-world use cases where causal terms are not explicitly specified. To ensure data quality, UniCo grounds answers with exact causal inference and filters cases with reasoning shortcuts. Upon supervised finetuning with 66.6K UniCo-generated instances, Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Instruct achieve an average of 22.9% improvements across all 18 in-distribution query types, and 8.1% over state-of-the-art causal data generation frameworks on 7 established causal benchmarks outside the training distribution. More importantly, in real-world medical understanding, legal decision, and tabular reasoning, UniCo-trained models consistently display more faithful reasoning traces, outperforming the base models by an average of 20.2% in faithfulness metrics. These suggest that causality-centered training not only strengthens causal reasoning, but also equips LLMs with a causal mindset in general reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [49] [DTO: a Differentiable Training Objective for Effective Counterfactual Story Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24885)
*Amelia Girard,Massimo Piccardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可微训练目标（DTO），通过端到端反向传播联合优化改写保真度与语义一致性，在反事实故事改写任务上超越了最大似然基线和基于偏好的方法，性能与当代大语言模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实故事改写中所需修改量小且高度局部化，传统最大似然训练容易忽略这些细微变化，而基于强化学习的方法训练缓慢且难以配置。

Method: 提出一种可微训练目标（DTO），对Transformer模型进行微调，通过端到端反向传播优化一个完全可微的损失函数，该损失函数同时奖励（i）对参考改写的保真度和（ii）与源叙事的语义一致性。

Result: 在TimeTravel和ART数据集上，DTO方法超越了最大似然基线和基于偏好的方法，与两个当代大语言模型相比在所有评估指标上表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 任务特定的可微训练目标对于细微、受控的文本生成任务具有有效性。

Abstract: Counterfactual story rewriting is a natural language processing task that requires updating an existing story to reflect a chosen alternative event, yet preserving all the unaffected storyline elements and overall coherence. While large language models have recently made remarkable progress on this task, it still remains challenging since the required modifications are typically very small in size and highly localized. As a consequence, models trained in a conventional manner with the maximum-likelihood training objective tend to overlook these nuances. At the same time, more sophisticated training approaches based on reinforcement learning are notoriously slow and difficult to set up. For these reasons, our paper proposes a novel, differentiable training objective (DTO) that directly optimizes for the requisite counterfactual improvements. In our approach, a transformer model is fine-tuned via end-to-end backpropagation against a fully differentiable loss function that jointly rewards (i) fidelity to the reference rewrite and (ii) semantic consistency with the source narrative. The empirical evaluation on the TimeTravel and ART datasets shows that the proposed DTO approach has been able to surpass a maximum-likelihood baseline and a preference-based approach, and perform competitively against two contemporary large language models in all evaluation metrics. These findings substantiate the effectiveness of task-specific differentiable objectives for nuanced, controlled text-generation tasks.

</details>


### [50] [When Reasoning Hurts: Source-Aware Evaluation of Frontier LLMs for Clinical SOAP Note Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24902)
*Faizan Faisal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究推理增强型LLM在结构化临床文档（SOAP笔记）生成中的表现，发现推理能力不仅未提升性能，反而显著降低了GPT-5.4的质量，提示需针对性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理增强型LLM在医学推理基准上表现优异，但能否将这种优势迁移到结构化的临床文档生成（如SOAP笔记）中尚不明确，为此本文进行了系统探究。

Method: 采用2x2受控实验设计，分别控制是否启用推理（provider-native reasoning）和是否使用同源检索增强生成（same-source RAG），在OMI Health、ACI-Bench和PriMock57三个数据集上评估GPT-5.4、DeepSeek-V4-Flash和Gemma-4-E4B，使用7项自动指标和2个参考感知LLM裁判。

Result: 非推理版本的GPT-5.4取得最高整体质量；DeepSeek-V4-Flash在推理增强模型中表现最佳；启用推理显著降低了GPT-5.4在所有数据集上的性能；同源RAG带来模型依赖的小幅改进。

Conclusion: 更强的推理能力不应被假设能自动提升对保真度敏感的SOAP笔记生成任务，需进行专门的、任务特定的评估。

Abstract: Reasoning-enabled LLMs perform strongly on medical reasoning benchmarks, but it remains unclear whether these gains transfer to structured clinical documentation; we investigate this question using SOAP note generation from clinical dialogue in a source-aware benchmark spanning OMI Health, ACI-Bench, and PriMock57. We evaluate GPT-5.4, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and Gemma-4-E4B in a controlled 2x2 design that independently toggles provider-native reasoning and same-source retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Outputs are assessed using seven automatic metrics alongside two reference-aware LLM judges. Both evaluation approaches agree that a non-reasoning GPT-5.4 configuration achieves the highest overall quality, while DeepSeek-V4-Flash performs best among reasoning-enabled configurations. Enabling reasoning significantly degrades GPT-5.4 performance across all three datasets, whereas same-source RAG yields smaller, model-dependent improvements. Overall, the findings indicate that stronger reasoning capability should not be assumed to improve fidelity-sensitive SOAP note generation without dedicated, task-specific evaluation.

</details>


### [51] [Quantifying the Impact of Translation Errors on Multilingual LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24904)
*Klaudia-Doris Thellmann,Bernhard Stadler,Michael Färber,Jens Lehmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 机器翻译基准中的翻译错误会明显降低多语言评估的准确性，且现有自动错误检测方法与人类判断的一致性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译基准广泛用于评估LLM的多语言能力，但其中的翻译错误对评估可靠性的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 使用LLM评委的自动MQM风格错误跨度检测和xCOMET-XXL基线，与专家人工标注进行比较，并分析翻译错误对准确率下降的影响。

Result: 自动错误跨度检测与人工标注的一致性较低；目标端翻译错误与准确率下降显著相关。

Conclusion: 需要更可靠的错误检测方法来确保多语言评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Machine-translated benchmarks are widely used to assess the multilingual capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet translation errors in these benchmarks remain underexplored, raising concerns about the reliability and comparability of multilingual evaluation. We address two practical gaps: (i) how well automatic MQM-style error spans from LLM judges and a span-aware QE baseline (xCOMET-XXL) match expert human span annotations on benchmark translations, and (ii) how strongly translation errors (as opposed to source-side issues in the English original) explain accuracy drops on translated benchmarks. We find that span agreement is non-trivial on naturally occurring benchmark translations, and that target-side translation errors are consistently associated with measurable, percentage-point drops in translated accuracy even after controlling for English correctness and source-side anomalies.

</details>


### [52] [Overview of the PsyDefDetect Shared Task at BioNLP 2026: Detecting Levels of Psychological Defense Mechanisms in Supportive Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24907)
*Hongbin Na,Zimu Wang,Zhaoming Chen,Yining Hua,Rena Gao,Kailai Yang,Ling Chen,Wei Wang,Shaoxiong Ji,John Torous,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PsyDefDetect共享任务，旨在从情感支持对话中检测心理防御机制水平，基于DMRS框架将求助者话语分为9类，最佳系统宏F1达0.420，仍有提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理防御机制是临床心理学中的重要概念，但NLP领域对其自动检测研究不足。现有工作缺乏细粒度、基于临床验证框架的对话级防御机制分类数据集和任务。

Method: 基于DMRS框架构建PsyDefConv语料库（200段对话、2336条求助者话语），组织CodaBench上的共享任务，包括7个层次DMRS等级和2个辅助标签，评估多系统性能。

Result: 172名参与者提交563次，21支团队正式注册；最佳系统宏F1为0.420，显著超越数据集论文中的最优微调基线，但仍有明显提升空间。

Conclusion: 任务揭示了三大挑战：对高适应类别的过度预测倾向、准确率与宏F1差距扩大反映的类别不平衡问题、以及理论感知和基于LLM的方法在细粒度防御功能分类中的价值。发布所有任务材料，呼吁社区继续研究。

Abstract: We present an overview of PsyDefDetect, the shared task on detecting levels of psychological defense mechanisms in emotional support dialogues, co-located with BioNLP@ACL 2026. Grounded in the clinically validated Defense Mechanism Rating Scales (DMRS) framework, the task asks systems to classify a target seeker utterance, given its preceding dialogue context, into one of nine categories: seven hierarchical DMRS levels plus two auxiliary labels. Participants worked on PsyDefConv, a newly released corpus of 200 dialogues and 2336 help-seeker utterances annotated under DMRS with substantial inter-annotator agreement. The task attracted 172 participants on CodaBench who produced 563 submissions, with 21 teams officially registering their results for the final ranking. The best system achieved a macro F1-score of 0.420, surpassing the strongest fine-tuned baseline reported in the dataset paper by a notable margin, yet leaving clear headroom. Our analysis highlights (i) a persistent tendency to over-predict the majority High-Adaptive class, (ii) a widening gap between accuracy and macro-F1 that reveals class-imbalance sensitivity, and (iii) the value of theory-aware and LLM-based approaches for fine-grained defensive-function classification. We release all task materials and invite the community to continue work on this novel intersection of clinical psychology and NLP.

</details>


### [53] [MultiHaluDet: Multilingual Hallucination Detection via LLM Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24919)
*Riasad Alvi,Nurul Labib Sayeedi,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MultiHaluDet框架，通过探测LLM全隐藏状态轨迹检测多语言幻觉，无需语言特定微调，在英语和非英语（法语、孟加拉语、阿姆哈拉语）场景均达SOTA性能（AUROC最高98.55%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM幻觉是可靠部署的关键障碍，尤其在非英语和资源受限情境下更为严重；现有检测方法依赖输出置信度或单层内部表征，难以捕捉跨语言的深层事实不一致性。

Method: 提出MultiHaluDet三阶段堆叠框架：1）提取冻结LLM多层隐藏状态轨迹序列特征；2）通过混合架构（多尺度注意力+自注意力池化）处理；3）生成折外嵌入，输入校准后的经典分类器集成进行检测。

Result: 在英语HaluEval和TriviaQA基准上达98.55% AUROC（Mistral-7B/LLaMA2-7B）；在法语（高资源）、孟加拉语（中资源）、阿姆哈拉语（低资源）上均超越基线，展现优异跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: MultiHaluDet无需语言特定微调即可有效检测多语言幻觉，在不同语言资源层级上均具鲁棒表征和迁移能力，为LLM安全部署提供可行方案。

Abstract: Hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) represent a critical barrier to their reliable deployment, a vulnerability heavily exacerbated in non-English and resource-constrained contexts. Existing detection approaches that rely on output confidence heuristics or single-layer internal representations frequently fail to capture deep, complex factual inconsistencies across diverse languages. To address this, we introduce MultiHaluDet, a novel three-stage stacking framework that detects multilingual hallucinations by probing the full hidden state trajectories of frozen LLMs without requiring language-specific fine-tuning. Our method extracts sequential features across multiple layers and processes them via a hybrid architecture using multi-scale attention and self-attention pooling. By generating out-of-fold embeddings that feed into a calibrated classical classifier ensemble, MultiHaluDet captures both fine-grained and coarse-grained patterns of factual inconsistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art detection performance, reaching up to 98.55% AUROC on the English HaluEval and TriviaQA benchmarks using Mistral-7B and LLaMA2-7B architectures. Crucially, we rigorously evaluate our framework's cross-lingual generalization across high (French), medium (Bangla), and low-resource (Amharic) languages. MultiHaluDet demonstrates exceptional representational robustness, consistently outperforming baselines and successfully transferring hallucination detection capabilities across typologically diverse linguistic tiers.

</details>


### [54] [H$^{2}$MT: Semantic Hierarchy-Aware Hierarchical Memory Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24930)
*Maryam Haghifam,Zifan He,Jason Cong,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: H²MT是一种通过构建语义层次结构，在推理时进行粗到细路由，实现长上下文高效推理的方法，在多个基准上取得了优于压缩、记忆令牌和检索增强生成方法的质量-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer模型处理长文本时面临上下文窗口有限、预填充延迟和内存随长度快速增长的问题；扁平化处理和分块检索会浪费计算资源在无关文本上；离线索引RAG引入外部存储管理开销且增加预填充成本。

Method: 离线构建语义层次结构，通过自底向上的后序聚合为每个节点计算记忆嵌入，推理时对查询进行粗到细路由，早期剪枝无关分支。

Result: 在LongBench QA（NarrativeQA, HotpotQA, QASPER）和两个结构化技术文档场景中，H²MT在ROUGE-L和F1上取得有竞争力的结果，同时峰值GPU内存和首次令牌时间（TTFT）低于提示压缩、记忆令牌方法和检索增强生成基线。

Conclusion: H²MT通过结构感知的长上下文推理，在质量与效率之间实现了有利的权衡，比现有方法更高效。

Abstract: Transformer-based LLMs achieve strong results on many language tasks; however, long inputs remain challenging because context windows are finite, and prefill latency and memory grow rapidly with prompt length. Flat token-stream processing and chunk-based retrieval can therefore spend substantial computation and context budget on text unrelated to the query. Offline-indexed RAG additionally introduces external storage and index management overhead, and typically appends retrieved evidence as raw text, increasing prefill cost and latency. H^{2}MT makes long-context inference structure-aware: it builds a semantic hierarchy offline, computes a memory embedding for each node via bottom-up post-order aggregation, and routes queries coarse-to-fine at inference to prune irrelevant branches early. On LongBench QA (NarrativeQA, HotpotQA, QASPER) and two structured technical-document settings, H MT achieves favorable quality efficiency trade-offs, delivering competitive ROUGE-L and F1 (where applicable) with lower peak GPU memory and time-to-first-token (TTFT) than prompt compression, memory-token methods, and retrieval-augmented generation baselines.

</details>


### [55] [NITP: Next Implicit Token Prediction for LLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24956)
*Xiangdong Zhang,Debing Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Xiaohan Qin,Yu Cheng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Next Implicit Token Prediction (NITP)方法，在标准下一词预测基础上增加连续表示空间的稠密监督，以解决潜在表示空间约束不足导致退化的问题，在0.5B-9B模型上显著提升性能且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准下一词预测仅通过输出logit空间的离散标签监督语言模型，这种稀疏的one-hot监督使得潜在表示空间约束不足，导致隐状态退化为各向异性的病态配置，限制了模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出Next Implicit Token Prediction (NITP)，在离散预测之外，额外在表示空间中添加稠密的连续监督。训练模型预测下一词的隐式语义内容，使用同一模型的浅层表示作为稳定的自监督目标。

Result: 在0.5B到9B参数的密集和MoE模型上，NITP持续提升下游性能。在9B MoE模型上，MMLU-Pro提升5.7%，C3提升6.4%，CommonsenseQA提升4.3%，仅增加约2%的训练FLOPs，无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: NITP通过表示空间的稠密正则化，有效缓解了标准NTP中潜在空间约束不足的问题，以极小代价显著提升语言模型性能，是一种实用且高效的训练增强方法。

Abstract: Standard next-token prediction (NTP) supervises language models solely through discrete labels in the output logit space. We argue that this sparse one-hot supervision leaves the latent representation space under-constrained, allowing hidden states to drift into degenerate and anisotropic configurations that can limit generalization. To address this issue, we propose Next Implicit Token Prediction (NITP), which augments discrete prediction with dense continuous supervision directly in the representation space. NITP trains the model to predict the implicit semantic content of the next token, using shallow-layer representations from the same model as stable self-supervised targets. We provide theoretical analysis showing that NITP regularizes the optimization landscape by mitigating under-constrained degrees of freedom and encouraging a compact, structured representation geometry. Empirically, across dense and MoE models ranging from 0.5B to 9B parameters, NITP consistently improves downstream performance with negligible computational overhead. On a 9B MoE model, NITP achieves a 5.7% absolute improvement on MMLU-Pro, along with gains of 6.4% on C3 and 4.3% on CommonsenseQA, with approximately 2% additional training FLOPs and no additional inference cost. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/aHapBean/NITP.

</details>


### [56] [SEP-Attack: A Simple and Effective Paradigm for Transfer-Based Textual Adversarial Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24958)
*Han Liu,Zhi Xu,Xiaotong Zhang,Feng Zhang,Xiaoming Xu,Wei Wang,Fenglong Ma,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SEP-Attack，一种基于行列式点过程（DPP）生成多样化集成权重、结合新置信度指标与可迁移性评分的高效文本对抗攻击方法，在多个数据集和真实API上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本可迁移对抗攻击方法存在两个核心问题：1）对多个替代子模型同等对待，未能区分各自的可迁移性差异；2）重要性分数估计不准确，导致攻击效果欠佳。

Method: 1）利用行列式点过程（DPP）生成多样化的替代模型集成权重；2）基于这些权重定义新的预测置信度指标，进而计算词重要性分数并生成对抗候选；3）量化每个候选的可迁移性评分，选择最优的作为最终可迁移对抗样本。

Result: 在四个数据集和两个真实API上的实验表明，SEP-Attack显著优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的SEP-Attack通过DPP多样化集成权重和可迁移性评分机制，有效提升了文本领域黑盒转移攻击的成功率，是一种简单而有效的范式。

Abstract: Despite the strong performance of deep neural networks in modern Web and language applications, they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, especially transferable attacks that generate adversarial examples using surrogate models without accessing the victim model. Transferable attacks in the text domain are still under-explored, with only a few studies addressing this challenging issue, often with suboptimal results due to equal treatment of submodels or inaccurate estimation of importance scores. To address these challenges, we propose a simple yet effective paradigm for transfer-based textual adversarial attack, named SEP-Attack. Specifically, we employ the Determinantal Point Process (DPP) to generate diverse surrogate ensemble weights, representing the transferability of submodels. Using these weights, we introduce a new metric to evaluate prediction confidence scores, which in turn are used to calculate word importance scores and generate adversarial candidates. Finally, we quantify the transferability score for each candidate and select the top ones as the final transferable adversarial examples. Experiments conducted on four datasets and two real-world APIs validate the efficacy of SEP-Attack, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [57] [Investigating the Interplay between Contextual and Parametric Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness under Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24960)
*Jingyi Sun,Qianli Wang,Pepa Atanasova,Nils Feldhus,Isabelle Augenstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FaithMate框架统一偏好对齐两种CoT忠实性范式（上下文忠实性与参数忠实性），发现两者正相关但不对称，且上下文忠实性内部指标存在权衡，表明CoT忠实性需多面优化与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅描述性比较CoT的两种忠实性范式（上下文忠实性与参数忠实性），缺乏统一的量化对比框架，无法揭示两者之间的相互作用关系。

Method: 提出FaithMate，一种统一的偏好对齐接口，用于优化模型朝向任一忠实性范式；通过三个模型、两个数据集和六个忠实性指标进行系统性实验分析。

Result: 两种范式正相关但不对称：优化参数忠实性可同时提升两个范式的指标，而优化上下文忠实性带来的增益更具波动性；上下文范式内部不同指标间的增益缺乏迁移性，存在内在权衡。

Conclusion: CoT忠实性并非单一目标，需要多维度的优化与评估策略。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) faithfulness, i.e., whether CoTs genuinely reflect large language models' (LLM) underlying behavior, is typically evaluated under two disjoint paradigms: contextual faithfulness, measured by perturbing the input or CoT trace, and parametric faithfulness, assessed by intervening on a model's parametric knowledge. Yet prior work compares them only descriptively. We fill this gap by proposing FaithMate, a unified preference-alignment interface for optimizing models towards either faithfulness paradigm. It enables us to investigate the interplay between the two paradigms, examining whether and to what extent faithfulness gains generalize within and across paradigms. Across three models, two datasets, and six faithfulness metrics, we find that the two paradigms are positively coupled, yet asymmetric: optimizing towards parametric faithfulness yields consistent gains across both paradigms, whereas the contextual counterpart delivers more variable gains. Within the contextual paradigm, faithfulness gains on one metric do not consistently transfer to others, implying that existing contextual metrics capture disjoint facets of faithfulness and exposing inherent trade-offs. These findings imply that CoT faithfulness is not a monolithic objective and therefore requires multifaceted optimization and evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [Large Language Model Selection with Limited Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24981)
*Yavuz Durmazkeser,Patrik Okanovic,Andreas Kirsch,Torsten Hoefler,Nezihe Merve Gürel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SELECT-LLM框架，通过主动选择最具信息量的查询样本来高效评估和选择最佳大语言模型，在23个数据集上实现最高84.78%的标注成本削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估依赖固定评测集上的昂贵人工标注，成本高且效率低，亟需一种能主动选择最具信息量查询样本来降低评估成本的模型选择方法。

Method: 基于期望信息增益设计查询选择规则，利用候选模型输出间的成对相似度计算信息量；仅依赖模型生成响应，无需架构假设或访问模型权重，适用于开源和闭源LLM。

Result: 在23个数据集、156个模型、多种任务和评估指标上，SELECT-LLM在所有设置中均优于最强基线，最佳模型选择标注成本降低81.8%，近最佳模型选择成本降低84.78%。

Conclusion: SELECT-LLM是首个面向LLM的主动模型选择框架，能显著降低标注成本的同时有效识别最佳模型，且适用于各类LLM（包括黑盒模型）。

Abstract: Choosing a Large Language Model (LLM) for a given task requires comparing many strong candidates, yet standard evaluation relies on costly annotations over fixed evaluation sets. To address this challenge, we develop SELECT-LLM, the first framework for active model selection of LLMs. SELECT-LLM aims to find a small set of queries whose annotations are most informative for identifying the best LLM for a given task. To this end, we introduce a query selection rule based on expected information gain, computed from pairwise similarities between candidate model outputs. Because this rule only uses generated model responses, SELECT-LLM can be applied across candidate models without assumptions about their architecture or access to model weights. This makes it suitable for both open-weight and black-box LLMs. We evaluate SELECT-LLM across 23 datasets, 156 evaluated models, diverse task families, and multiple text evaluation metrics. Across all experiments, SELECT-LLM improves over the strongest baseline in every setting, with annotation cost reductions up to 81.8% for best model selection and up to 84.78% for near-best model selection.

</details>


### [59] [Exploring Profiles of Cognitive Distortions Associated with Mental Health Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24996)
*Alina Anikejeva,Kairit Sirts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过Reddit数据分析了多种心理健康状况下的认知扭曲特征，发现心理健康群体普遍比对照组表现出更高的认知扭曲水平，且不同疾病间的扭曲模式大致相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算心理健康研究主要关注抑郁症中的认知扭曲，缺乏对多种心理健康状况下扭曲特征的系统比较。

Method: 使用基于n-gram的方法和微调Transformer模型，分析了一个包含九个自我报告心理健康群体和对照组的Reddit数据集。

Result: 心理健康群体（无论是合并分析还是单独分析）的认知扭曲发生率均高于对照组，效应量从小到中等；各群体间扭曲模式大致相似，但部分群体整体扭曲水平更高。

Conclusion: 简单的词汇分析方法可用于大规模心理健康文本数据的群体层面探索性分析。

Abstract: Cognitive distortions, distorted patterns of thinking, have been increasingly studied in computational mental health research. Although they are related to many, if not all, mental health disorders, most existing studies focus primarily on depression. In this work, we explore distortion profiles across multiple mental health conditions. We analyzed a large Reddit-based dataset containing posts from nine self-reported mental health groups as well as a control group using both an n-gram-based method and a fine-tuned transformer model for detecting cognitive distortions. Mental health groups, both when pooled together and when examined individually, showed higher prevalence of cognitive distortions compared to the control group, with the effect sizes ranging from small to moderate. When comparing distortion profiles across conditions, we observed largely similar patterns, although some groups exhibited overall higher levels of distortions than others. These findings suggest that relatively simple lexical approaches can be useful for exploratory analyses of group-level trends in large-scale mental health text data.

</details>


### [60] [Better, Faster: Harnessing Self-Improvement in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24998)
*Qihuang Zhong,Liang Ding,Juhua Liu,Bo Du,Leszek Rutkowski,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HSIR框架，通过验证后退出采样策略缓解数据不平衡，并设计内在多样性分数抑制过度思考，从而提升大推理模型自我训练的效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进训练方法在复杂推理任务中常导致模型崩溃，原因是训练数据存在不平衡（简单样本多、困难样本少）以及过度思考（冗余推理步骤）两大问题。

Method: 提出HSIR框架，包含两个核心策略：(1) verify-then-exit采样策略，高效收集困难问题的准确解以缓解数据不平衡；(2) 内在多样性分数(Intrinsic Diversity score)，量化并过滤过度思考的解。此外，提出H-GRPO算法，将内在多样性作为外部奖励，通过强化学习鼓励简洁多样的推理。

Result: HSIR在多种后训练范式下实现最高+10.9%的平均性能提升，同时推理效率提升，相对推理开销降低最高42.4%。

Conclusion: HSIR有效解决了自我改进训练中的两大关键问题，在提升推理性能的同时显著降低推理成本，验证了该方法在复杂推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Self-improvement training enables the large reasoning models (LRMs) to improve themselves by self-generating reasoning trajectories as training data without external supervision. However, we find that this method often falls short in complex reasoning tasks and even leads to model collapse. Through a series of preliminary analyses, we reveal two problems: (1) data imbalance, where most training samples are simple, but the challenging yet crucial samples are scarce; (2) overthinking, where many undesired samples with redundant reasoning steps are used for self-training. To this end, we propose HSIR, which effectively Harnesses Self-Improvement in large Reasoning models via two simple-yet-effective approaches. Specifically, HSIR introduces a verify-then-exit sampling strategy to mitigate data imbalance by efficiently collecting more accurate solutions for difficult queries, and designs an Intrinsic Diversity score to quantify overthinking and filter out the undesired solutions. We apply HSIR to various post-training paradigms, among which we further propose H-GRPO, an enhanced GRPO algorithm that leverages the intrinsic diversity as an external reward to encourage concise and diverse reasoning via reinforcement learning. Extensive results show that HSIR not only effectively enhances the reasoning performance, i.e., bringing up to +10.9% average performance gains, but also significantly improves the reasoning efficiency by reducing up to 42.4% relative inference overhead.

</details>


### [61] [Language Bias in LVLMs: From In-Depth Analysis to Simple and Effective Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25036)
*Yangneng Chen,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型视觉语言模型中的语言偏差问题，发现其根源在于训练过程中的模态不对齐，并提出了两种简单有效的方法（LBR和LBP）来缓解语言偏差，提升模型的多模态理解能力和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLMs存在幻觉问题，即输出流畅但图像不一致。先前研究将原因归咎于语言偏差（模型过度依赖文本而忽视视觉输入），但多停留在经验分析层面，未揭示其根本原因。本文旨在系统研究语言偏差的根源并提出有效缓解方法。

Method: 1. 系统性分析发现语言偏差根源于训练过程中的模态不对齐（VIT和DPO都倾向于优先优化文本能力）。2. 提出语言偏差正则化（LBR），在指令微调中通过正则化缓解语言偏差。3. 提出语言偏差惩罚（LBP），在DPO训练过程中惩罚语言偏差。

Result: LBR在十余个通用基准上持续提升性能；LBP显著减少幻觉并提高可信度。两种方法均无需引入额外数据或辅助模型。

Conclusion: 所提方法不仅有效缓解了语言偏差，还提升了LVLMs的整体对齐能力，为多模态模型的训练提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) extend large language models with visual understanding, but remain vulnerable to hallucination, where outputs are fluent yet inconsistent with images. Recent studies link this issue to language bias-the tendency of LVLMs to over-rely on text while neglecting visual inputs. Yet most analyses remain empirical without uncovering its underlying cause. In this paper, we provide a systematic study of language bias and identify its root in modality misalignment during training. Our analysis shows that both Visual Instruction Tuning (VIT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) often prioritize textual improvements, which may cause LVLMs to overly lean toward language modeling rather than balanced multimodal understanding. To address this, we propose two simple yet effective methods: Language Bias Regularization (LBR) which mitigates language bias through regularization during instruction tuning, and Language Bias Penalty (LBP), which penalizes language bias in the DPO training process. Extensive experiments across diverse models and benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. LBR consistently improves performance on over ten general benchmarks, while LBP significantly reduces hallucination and improves trustworthiness. Together, these methods not only mitigate language bias but also advance the overall alignment of LVLMs, all without introducing any additional data or auxiliary models. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/lab-klc/LVLM-Language-Bias.

</details>


### [62] [TRACE: A taxonomy-grounded synthetic dataset for teaching-program generation and session interpretation in Applied Behavior Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25038)
*Festus Kahunla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRACE数据集，包含2,999个通过确定性分类法驱动生成器生成的合成ABA临床示例，用于教学程序生成和多会话行为解释两个任务，并公开了数据集和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABA临床文档数据受HIPAA和保密规则保护，无法发布真实训练语料，因此需要合成数据来支持相关NLP研究。

Method: 基于经典ABA文献构建确定性分类法驱动生成器，自动生成涵盖两任务（教学程序生成、多会话行为解释）的2,999个合成示例，并提供完整的采样来源追溯。

Result: 生成了2,999个示例，划分为训练(2,549)、验证(149)、测试(281)和健全性检查(20)四个子集，以CC BY-NC 4.0和MIT许可证发布。

Conclusion: TRACE是一个研究性工件，尚未经过临床验证，但其分类法驱动的方法为ABA领域提供了可复现的合成训练数据生成范式。

Abstract: Applied Behavior Analysis (ABA) is a clinical discipline whose documentation, teaching programs and multi-session behavioral logs, is formulaic and high-volume, yet real session data is HIPAA-protected and bound by professional confidentiality rules, blocking the release of a training corpus. We present TRACE (Taxonomy-Referenced ABA Clinical Examples), a 2,999-example synthetic instruction-tuning dataset covering two ABA tasks: teaching-program generation across Discrete Trial Training, Natural Environment Teaching, and Task Analysis; and multi-session behavioral interpretation across twelve trajectory patterns and thirteen target behaviors. Every example is produced by a deterministic taxonomy-driven generator grounded in the canonical ABA literature, and every example carries complete sampling provenance, the exact taxonomy cells that produced it. The dataset is released under CC BY-NC 4.0 for data and MIT for code, with stratified train (2,549), validation (149), test (281), and sanity (20) splits. TRACE is a research artifact and has not been clinically validated.

</details>


### [63] [Faithfulness Metrics Don't Measure Faithfulness: A Meta-Evaluation with Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25052)
*Yoav Gur-Arieh,Ana Marasović,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了BonaFide基准测试，包含3,066个带真实忠实性标签的思维链样本，系统评估了现有忠实性度量方法，发现大多数指标表现接近随机水平，存在预测偏差，且对长思维链效果下降，揭示了当前忠实性评估的严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链忠实性度量指标缺乏真实标签验证，因为模型内部计算不可直接观察；已有基准依赖于近似指标（如合理性或重要性），这些属性与忠实性正交，可能导致误导性结论。

Method: 通过构建内部计算结果可观测的任务，设计自动化标注管线，在步骤级别和思维链级别生成真实忠实性标签，构建了包含13个任务、10个模型、3,066个标注样本的BonaFide基准。

Result: 大多数忠实性度量指标表现接近随机水平，存在强烈预测偏差，在长思维链上性能退化；最佳指标在思维链级别仅达0.70 AUROC，步骤级别最佳仅0.59 AUROC，且无法跨设置迁移，计算成本过高。

Conclusion: 当前忠实性评估存在根本性缺陷，迫切需要开发更可靠且高效的思维链忠实性度量方法。

Abstract: Chains of thought (CoTs) have become central in interpreting and auditing behaviors of large language models. Yet growing evidence suggests that these traces often fail to faithfully represent the computations behind a model's predictions. Several faithfulness metrics have been proposed, but whether they indeed measure faithfulness remains unknown. Answering this requires ground-truth labels, which are hard to obtain since internal computations are not directly observable. Consequently, most works proposing metrics report only absolute scores or comparisons to prior metrics, and the few existing benchmarks rely on proxies like plausibility or importance, properties orthogonal to faithfulness that can mislead about whether a CoT can be trusted. We address this challenge by constructing tasks whose outputs reveal which intermediate computations must have produced them, and developing an automated labeling pipeline that yields ground-truth faithfulness labels at both the step and CoT level. Building on this methodology, we present BonaFide, a benchmark of 3,066 labeled CoTs across 13 tasks and 10 models, and use it to conduct the first systematic evaluation of prominent faithfulness metrics. Our experiments show that most metrics perform near chance, exhibit strong prediction biases and degrade on longer CoTs. The best metric reaches only 0.70 AUROC at the CoT level while another reaches 0.59 at the step level, with neither transferring across settings, while entailing prohibitively high computational cost. Our results expose fundamental gaps in current faithfulness evaluation and call for the development of more reliable and efficient metrics.

</details>


### [64] [STREAM: A Data-Centric Framework for Mining High-Value Task-Oriented Dialogues from Streaming Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25162)
*Liang Xue,Haoyu Liu,Cheng Wang,Pengyu Chen,Haozhuo Zheng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Stream框架，利用流媒体（直播/短视频）合成垂直领域任务型对话数据集StreamDial（87K会话/1.5M轮次），解决数据稀缺的三难困境，在汽车、餐饮、酒店三个领域实现高质量对话生成，并提升对话状态追踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直领域大语言模型受限于复杂、领域特定的任务型对话数据稀缺。现有数据获取面临三难困境：专家标注昂贵、真实服务对话受隐私和商业限制、静态语料库时效性差。

Method: 提出Stream框架，从流媒体（直播/短视频）中挖掘真实交互信号，通过角色落地的人物构建与对话蓝图构建结合来合成对话，并采用RAG支持知识感知响应。数据集结构化为四元组⟨P_u, P_a, B, H⟩。

Result: 构建了StreamDial数据集，包含87,498个对话会话和1,497,320轮次，平均17.11轮/会话，覆盖汽车、餐饮、酒店三个领域。自动评估和下游任务表明StreamDial在对话质量和对话状态追踪上优于强基线，并展示了多语言迁移能力。

Conclusion: Stream框架有效解决了垂直领域对话数据稀缺问题，通过流媒体合成的高质量数据集能够提升模型性能，且具有良好的跨领域和多语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models for vertical domains are bottlenecked by the scarcity of complex, domain-specific task-oriented dialogues. Existing data acquisition pipelines face a persistent trilemma: expert annotation is expensive, real-world service conversations are constrained by privacy and commercial restrictions, and static corpora quickly become temporally stale. We propose Stream, a data-centric framework that leverages publicly available streaming media (live streams and short videos) to synthesize high-value service dialogues at scale. Stream mines authentic interaction signals from noisy streams and synthesizes conversations by integrating role-grounded persona construction with Conversational Blueprint construction; it further adopts retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to support knowledge-aware responses. Based on Stream, we release StreamDial, a large-scale multi-domain dataset covering Automotive, Restaurant, and Hotel. StreamDial contains 87,498 dialogue sessions and 1,497,320 turns in total, with an average of 17.11 turns per session and a comparable scale across domains. Each session is organized as a structured quadruplet $\langle P_u, P_a, B, H \rangle$ that pairs dialogue history with explicit user/agent personas and a Conversational Blueprint, capturing realistic service behaviors such as requirement mining, constraint conflicts, negotiation, and recovery. Evaluations with automatic judges and downstream tasks show that StreamDial improves intrinsic dialogue quality over strong baselines, and models trained with StreamDial improve Dialogue State Tracking across backbones; we further report a completed human-evaluation set and encouraging multilingual transfer on Qwen3-8B under a controlled training budget. The data is released in https://github.com/hitxueliang/DialogDataSetBySTREAM.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [65] [Confidence Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23909)
*Noam Michael,Daniel BenShushan,Jacob Bien,Don A. Moore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究LLM校准性，发现LLM像人一样过度自信，尤其在困难任务上；而简单任务反而表现出信心不足。开发了LifeEval测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大语言模型在不同任务上的置信度校准性，探究其是否像人类一样存在过度自信倾向。

Method: 通过预注册研究，比较LLM的置信度与实际准确率，并开发LifeEval测试集评估不同难度级别下的模型校准性。

Result: LLM平均而言过度自信（置信度高于准确率）；存在困难-简单效应：困难任务过度自信显著，简单任务则显著信心不足。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在置信度校准上存在系统性偏差，受任务难度调节，LifeEval可用于评估此问题。

Abstract: We investigate the calibration of large language models' (LLMs') confidence across diverse tasks. The results of our preregistered study show that the current crop of LLMs are, like people, too sure they are right: confidence exceeds accuracy, on average. Importantly, however, this tendency is moderated by a powerful hard-easy effect, wherein overconfidence is greatest on difficult tests; by contrast, easy tests actually show substantial underconfidence. We develop LifeEval, a test for evaluating model calibration across levels of difficulty.

</details>


### [66] [How Much Thinking is Enough? Quantifying and Understanding Redundancy in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23926)
*Zhiyuan Zhai,Xinkai You,Wenjing Yan,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文量化了推理型大语言模型思考过程中的冗余度（61%-93%），并从理论上证明这种冗余是长度无关结果奖励的结构性必然结果，而非模型特定缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管推理模型会生成超长的思维链，但其中大量重述、验证和自我反思究竟有多少是必要的，从未被大规模量化或从第一性原理解释。

Method: 形式化定义推理冗余度（可截断的尾随分段步骤最大比例，同时模型仍能输出正确答案），在4个前沿推理模型和2个数学基准上大规模量化，并进行理论证明。

Result: 步骤级冗余度高达61%-93%，中位关键前缀仅为一个分段步骤；在MATH-500上所有模型在最难Level-5问题上仍保持46%-85%的冗余度。

Conclusion: 冗余是长度无关结果奖励的结构性后果，而非模型特定缺陷；过度思考是当前推理模型训练方式的固有属性。

Abstract: Reasoning-capable large language models solve hard problems by emitting long chains of thought, paying heavily in latency, GPU time, and energy. Casual inspection of their traces reveals extensive reformulation, verification, and circular self-reflection, yet how much of this deliberation is actually necessary has never been measured at scale or explained from first principles. This paper closes both gaps.
  We formalise reasoning redundancy directly in terms of the reasoning model itself: the redundancy of a correct trace is the largest fraction of its trailing segmented steps that can be truncated while $π$, forced to terminate thinking and emit a final answer, still produces the correct answer. A large-scale quantification across four frontier reasoning models and two mathematical benchmarks shows that step-level redundancy is consistently high -- between 61% and 93% across the 8 (model, benchmark) conditions we study, with the median critical prefix equal to a single segmented step in six of the eight conditions -- that the finding is robust to the choice of judge family, and that although $ρ$ decreases with problem difficulty on MATH-500, all four models remain substantially redundant ($ρ\in [46\%, 85\%]$) even on the hardest Level-5 problems.
  We then prove that this redundancy is a structural consequence of length-agnostic outcome rewards, not a model-specific artefact: under any such reward, no finite expected stopping time is optimal. The result holds regardless of RL algorithm, base model, data distribution, or whether the policy is obtained via RL or distillation; over-thinking is therefore not a bug to be patched in individual models but a structural property of how current reasoning models are trained. Code: https://github.com/zhiyuanZhai20/how-much-thinking-is-enough

</details>


### [67] [Toward Reliable Design of LLM-Enabled Agentic Workflows: Optimizing Latency-Reliability-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23929)
*Ya-Ting Yang,Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了LLM使能的智能体工作流中延迟、可靠性和成本之间的基本权衡，提出了性能模型，并给出了在延迟和成本约束下的最优工作流设计方法，包括水填充令牌分配策略和影子价格表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统越来越多地依赖于由多个交互智能体组成的工作流，其中一些由大语言模型驱动，另一些由传统计算模块驱动。需要理解这种混合系统中延迟、可靠性和成本之间的基本权衡关系。

Method: 为LLM和非LLM智能体建立性能模型，捕捉计算努力与输出质量之间的关系；对LLM智能体使用参数指数可靠性函数建模推理和输出令牌的影响；在延迟和成本约束下研究顺序工作流的设计。

Result: 提出了水填充令牌分配策略，并给出了以影子价格形式表征的最优工作流可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM使能的智能体工作流提供了理论分析框架，揭示了延迟、可靠性和成本之间的权衡关系，并给出了最优资源配置策略。

Abstract: Modern AI systems increasingly rely on workflows composed of multiple interacting agents, some powered by large language models (LLMs) and others by conventional computational modules. This paper analyzes the fundamental tradeoffs between latency, reliability, and cost in LLM-enabled agentic workflows. We introduce performance models for both LLM and non-LLM agents that capture the relationship between computational effort and output quality, incorporating the impact of reasoning and output tokens for LLM agents using a parametric exponential reliability function. Then, we study the design of sequential workflows under latency and cost constraints. Main results include a water-filling token allocation policy and characterizations of optimal workflow reliability in terms of shadow prices.

</details>


### [68] [BODHI: Precise OS Kernel Specification Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23931)
*Zhiming Chang,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BODHI方法，通过领域知识提示（结构化C到Python翻译指南）提升大语言模型在操作系统内核形式化验证规格生成任务上的性能，在OSV-Bench基准上将最佳Pass@1从55.10%提升至96.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统内核的形式化验证需要精确的系统调用规格说明，但手工编写这些规格说明需要深厚的领域专业知识，因此希望利用大语言模型实现自动化，然而现有方法在OSV-Bench基准上最好Pass@1仅为55.10%，性能不足。

Method: 提出BODHI领域知识提示方法，在标准少样本提示中增加结构化的C到Python翻译指南，涵盖15类领域特定的翻译模式。受结构化思维链（SCoT）启发，按关注点分离原则组织，分别处理前置条件提取和后置条件生成。

Result: 在6家供应商的9种模型上，BODHI均提升性能（+11%至+32%），最佳配置（Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI）达到96.73% Pass@1。同时减少了语法和语义错误，对指令遵循能力强的模型效果最显著。

Conclusion: 领域知识注入是一种模型无关的技术，能有效弥合通用代码生成与形式化规格合成之间的差距。

Abstract: The formal verification of operating system kernels requires precise specifications that capture the intended behavior of system calls. Writing these specifications manually demands deep domain expertise, motivating the use of large language models (LLMs) to automate the process. However, in OSV-Bench, a benchmark of 245 specification generation tasks derived from the Hyperkernel OS kernel, the best reported Pass@1 is 55.10%. We propose a domain knowledge prompting method (BODHI), which augments the standard few-shot prompt with a structured C-to-Python translation guide covering 15 categories of domain-specific translation patterns. Inspired by Structured Chain-of-Thought (SCoT) prompting, the guide organizes translation by separation of concerns, addressing pre-condition extraction and post-condition generation as distinct categories. Evaluated on nine models from six providers (Anthropic, Mistral, Amazon, DeepSeek, Meta, Alibaba), covering dense, mixture-of-experts and reasoning architectures, BODHI improves every model tested, with gains ranging from +11% to +32%. The best configuration (Claude Opus 4.6 + BODHI) reaches 96.73% Pass@1. BODHI reduces both syntax and semantic errors, with the strongest effect on models that have sufficient instruction-following capability to utilize structured reference material. These results demonstrate that domain knowledge injection is a model-agnostic technique that substantially bridges the gap between general-purpose code generation and formal specification synthesis.

</details>


### [69] [When Correct Beliefs Collapse: Epistemic Resilience of LLMs under Clinical Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23932)
*Boyu Xiao,Xiuqi Tian,Xuwen Song,Haochun Wang,Guanchun Song,Sendong Zhao,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Med-Stress压力测试框架评估LLM在临床对话中的信念稳定性，发现知识与鲁棒性存在分离，并提出RBED和R-FT两种防御方法提升模型抗压能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在临床对话中存在严重的多轮谄媚现象（sy舐病），即在医患对话中面对持续施压时，模型会放弃初始正确的诊断，这暴露了模型知识准确性与信念稳定性之间的差距，亟需系统性的评估与缓解方法。

Method: 1) Med-Stress：设计渐进式施压的临床对话压力测试框架；2) RBED：基于角色的轻量级推理时防御方法；3) R-FT：面向鲁棒性的微调训练方法，让模型内化基于证据的抗压能力。

Result: 1) 9个前沿LLM中观察到知识与鲁棒性显著分离，高诊断能力不意味着高信念稳定性；2) 部分模型存在巨大的知识-鲁棒性差距；3) R-FT几乎完全消除信念改变，显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM在临床对话中需要同时关注医学知识准确性和信念鲁棒性，R-FT是一种有效的训练时防御方法，可大幅缓解多轮谄媚问题。

Abstract: Despite strong medical benchmark accuracy, LLMs can exhibit severe multi-turn sycophancy in clinical dialogue, abandoning initial correct diagnosis under escalating pressure. We propose \textbf{\textsc{Med-Stress}}, a targeted stress test framework that evaluates belief stability under escalating pressure. Across nine frontier large language models (LLMs), we find a clear dissociation between medical knowledge and robustness: high initial diagnostic capability does not imply high belief stability, yielding large knowledge-robustness gaps for several LLMs. To mitigate this failure mode, we propose a lightweight inference-time defense, \textbf{\texttt{RBED}} (\textbf{R}ole-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{E}pistemic \textbf{D}efense), and \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} (\textbf{R}esilience-oriented \textbf{F}ine-\textbf{T}uning), a training-time approach that internalizes evidence-based resistance to pressure. Experiments show that \textbf{\texttt{R-FT}} nearly eliminates belief change and substantially improves robustness.

</details>


### [70] [Practical Quantum CIM Empowerment via All-Domestic-Core Agentic Large Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23934)
*Wang Rui,Lu Diannan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将飞秒激光泵浦的相干伊辛机（CIM）与基于LLM的智能体系统（LangGraph/LangChain框架）相结合，实现了QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重决策迭代和文献方案快速验证等任务的自动化。所有工作均基于国产大模型和国产CIM硬件完成，真正实现了全自主可控的量子CIM实用赋能，并发现了智能体与量子计算迭代互促的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子计算建模对非专家门槛高、专家需要反复迭代约束权重和建模方法的问题，降低使用难度和人力成本。

Method: 将飞秒激光泵浦的CIM与基于LangGraph和LangChain框架的LLM驱动智能体系统集成，实现QUBO/Ising模型校准、约束权重决策迭代及文献方案快速验证。

Result: LLM能够有效完成量子建模任务，且全部基于国产大模型和国产CIM硬件实现全自主可控的技术集成，同时发现智能体辅助量子计算迭代可反向增强智能体自身问题解决能力的新范式。

Conclusion: 实现了大模型与量子计算两大前沿领域的稳健技术集成，为后续研究奠定基础，但仍面临当前阶段的持续性挑战，同时发现了二者互促增强的新范式。

Abstract: Quantum computing devices are recognized as powerful tools for solving NP-complete problems. However, the intricacy of their modeling presents notable barriers for non-specialists, while the tedious iteration of constraint weights and modeling methodologies also consumes substantial effort on the part of experts. To address these challenges, this study integrates a femtosecond laser-pumped Coherent Ising Machine (CIM) with an LLM-driven agentic system by leveraging the LangGraph and LangChain frameworks. Comprehensive investigations demonstrate that large language models (LLMs) can effectively perform such tasks in modeling as QUBO/Ising model calibration, constraint weight decision iteration and rapid validation of literature-reported schemes. Notably, all these tasks can be fully implemented based on domestic large models, combined with domestically developed CIM hardware, we truly achieve the practical empowerment of quantum CIM that fully relies on all-domestic agentic large models and hardware. This work successfully realizes robust technological integration, laying a solid foundation for subsequent research. Nevertheless, it also identifies the persisting challenges in the two cutting-edge fields of large models and quantum computing at the current stage. Encouragingly, we unexpectedly discover a promising new paradigm where accumulated knowledge from agent-assisted quantum computing iterations reciprocally enhances the agent's own problem-solving capability, thereby addressing these challenges.

</details>


### [71] [BoxLitE: A Faithful Knowledge Base Embedding Based on Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23937)
*Bruno F. Lourenço,Hesham Morgan,Ana Ozaki,Aleksandar Pavlović,Emanuel Sallinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BoxLitE，一种针对DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$知识库的嵌入模型，通过凸优化实现弱忠实嵌入，能同时表示ABox事实和TBox概念层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识库嵌入方法虽能用凸区域表示概念层次，但凸性在训练中未被充分利用，缺乏形式化保证。

Method: 提出BoxLitE模型，将概念映射为轴对齐的盒子（box）区域，将知识库嵌入任务转化为凸优化问题。

Result: 证明了对任意可满足的DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$知识库，都存在一个弱忠实的BoxLitE嵌入。

Conclusion: BoxLitE首次将KB嵌入建模为凸优化问题，并保证了弱忠实性，弥合了知识图谱嵌入与本体推理之间的差距。

Abstract: Knowledge base (KB) embeddings aim at combining the capability of classical knowledge graph embeddings to generalize the information present in facts, the ABox, with conceptual knowledge represented in an ontology language, the TBox. Several authors have recently explored the idea of mapping concepts to convex regions in a vector space. This is useful to represent hierarchies, typically present in TBoxes, since more general concepts can be mapped to larger regions, containing those regions associated with more specific concepts. However, the power of convexity is rarely leveraged during the actual learning tasks. Here, we introduce BoxLitE, a KB embedding model for DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ that allows for convex optimization. We show that for any satisfiable DL-Lite$^{\mathcal{H}}$ KB, there is a BoxLitE embedding that is a weakly faithful model. As a proof of concept, we show how to formulate the KB embedding task as a convex optimization problem and how to obtain embeddings with such desirable faithfulness properties.

</details>


### [72] [Authority Inversion in LLM-Mediated Ubiquitous Systems: When Models Trust Users Over Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23938)
*Long Zhang,Zi-bo Qin,Wei-neng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在多模态融合中存在"权威反转"现象：数值传感器数据被忽视，自然语言用户主张主导决策。提出几何框架、审计指标（CIR/AAI）和推理时层干预方法GAC，在576个冲突实例上验证，将HAR准确率从0-1.6%提升至21.9-27.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在融合传感器测量值与用户主张时，隐式权威分配机制未得到检验，存在物理传感数据被忽视的安全隐患，亟需揭示并解决这一可靠性问题。

Method: 构建上下文融合的几何框架，提出两个可计算审计指标（上下文融合比CIR、权威对齐指数AAI），并设计推理时层级干预方法GAC（几何权威校准），通过理论指导的因果注入修正错误决策。

Result: 四个模型（4B-35B参数）在四个数据集共576个冲突实例上，数值任务中传感器信任度极低（AAI=-0.805, Cohen's d=-2.14）；GAC将HAR准确率从0-1.6%提升至21.9-27.5%，80.2%错误决策被翻转（随机控制<0.4%）。

Conclusion: LLM中介系统中的权威分配必须被显式审计和按应用配置，而非保持隐式。格式依赖性（数值vs自然语言）是权威反转的关键因素。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly fuse heterogeneous inputs in ubiquitous systems. Yet, how LLMs implicitly allocate authority when sensor measurements and user claims conflict remains unexamined, raising critical reliability concerns for deployments where physical sensing must retain priority. Unlike explicit traditional fusion, LLMs bury authority allocation within learned representations. We discover this allocation is severely format-dependent: numerical sensor data fails to integrate into answer-relevant model directions, allowing natural-language claims to dominate the final decision, a phenomenon we term \textbf{Authority Inversion}.To diagnose and mitigate this, we develop a geometric framework of context integration, introduce two computable audit metrics, specifically the Context Integration Ratio (CIR) and Authority Alignment Index (AAI), and propose Geometric Authority Calibration (GAC), an inference-time layer-level intervention to suppress misplaced user authority. Evaluating four models (4B to 35B parameters, three architectures) across four datasets totaling 576 conflict instances reveals extreme inversion: on numerical tasks, models exhibit near-zero sensor trust (AAI = -0.805, Cohen's d = -2.14), unaffected by model capacity. Validating our geometric framework, theory-guided causal injection flips 80.2\% of incorrect decisions (vs. <0.4\% for random controls). Practically, GAC improves HAR accuracy from 0 -- 1.6\% to 21.9 -- 27.5\%, outperforming prompting baselines. Ultimately, authority allocation in LLM-mediated systems must be explicitly audited and application-specifically configured rather than left implicit.

</details>


### [73] [DRIVE: Modeling Skills at the Reasoning and Interaction Levels for Web Agents under Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23939)
*Xirui Liu,Sihang Zhou,Yanning Hou,Rong Zhou,Haoyuan Chen,Maolin He,Siwei Wang,Hao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRIVE提出将Web智能体的历史经验解耦为可迁移的自然语言推理技能和可执行的程序化交互技能，通过场景感知协调机制和技能级反思，显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在存储Web智能体经验时，将抽象推理知识（可跨网站迁移）与具体交互知识（依赖页面特定上下文）混在一起，导致抽象表示无法在具体页面上执行，而具体表示又无法跨领域泛化，限制了能力积累。

Method: 提出DRIVE双层级技能建模框架：(1) 将历史经验分离为自然语言推理技能（可迁移任务逻辑）和程序化交互技能（可执行操作）；(2) 场景感知协调机制根据任务语义自适应检索和调用双层级技能；(3) 技能级反思识别层级特定的失败模式，定向扩展和优化技能库。

Result: 在五个WebArena领域上，任务成功率达52.8%，比无技能基线高出7.3个百分点。消融实验表明推理技能和交互技能提供了不同且互补的收益。

Conclusion: 分离可迁移的任务逻辑与可执行的页面级操作是有效的，支持了Web智能体能力的定向积累与泛化。

Abstract: Web agents require both high-level reasoning (for task decomposition) and low-level interactions (for page elements manipulation) to conduct different tasks. However, these knowledge types differ fundamentally: reasoning knowledge (e.g., booking a flight requires first searching for routes) is abstract and transferable across websites, while interaction knowledge (e.g., clicking the Search button at a specific coordinate on Site A) depends heavily on page-specific contexts. Existing methods store experiences uniformly. This creates a dilemma: abstract representations lose executability on concrete pages, while concrete representations fail to generalize across domains. This entanglement limits capability accumulation: on new websites, agents either fail to recognize reusable task logic due to surface-level differences or attempt infeasible actions from outdated page structures. To disentangle them, we propose DRIVE, a dual-level skill modeling framework separating historical experience into natural language reasoning skills, which capture transferable task logic, and programmatic interaction skills, grounding abstract actions to executable operations. A scene-aware coordination mechanism adaptively retrieves and invokes these dual-level skills based on task semantics. DRIVE also uses skill-level reflection to identify hierarchy-specific failure modes, enabling targeted skill library expansion and refinement. Experiments across five WebArena domains show DRIVE attains an average task success rate of 52.8%, exceeding the skill-free baseline by 7.3 percentage points. Further ablations show reasoning and interaction skills provide distinct, complementary benefits, supporting separation of transferable task logic from executable page-level operations.

</details>


### [74] [Residual Drift Dominates Contradiction in Multi-Turn Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23940)
*Sebastien Kawada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DRIFT-Bench基准，发现多轮推理系统的主要失败模式是“可满足漂移”（状态一致但答案违反先前承诺），而非逻辑矛盾。MUS-Repair方法表现最佳，但残差错误98-100%为可满足漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮推理系统如何失败？预期答案是逻辑矛盾（系统状态不可满足）。本文旨在揭示实际的主要失败模式。

Method: 构建DRIFT-Bench基准（816个测试问题，3个约束域），评估4种方法在4个开源模型上的表现。提出MUS-Repair方法，利用最小不可满足子集反馈给生成器。

Result: MUS-Repair在所有设置下表现最强（比最佳非MUS基线高1.8-15.0个百分点）。残差错误中98-100%为可满足漂移，矛盾几乎降至零。

Conclusion: 可靠的多轮系统需要单独验证返回的答案是否尊重已维护的状态。

Abstract: How do multi-turn reasoning systems fail? The expected answer is logical contradiction, in which the system's maintained state becomes unsatisfiable. We show that the dominant mode is instead satisfiable drift, where the internal state stays consistent while the returned answer silently violates prior commitments. We build DRIFT-Bench (Decomposing Reasoning Into Failure Types), a solver-instrumented benchmark of 816 test problems across three constraint domains, and evaluate four methods on it across four open-weight models (8B-120B parameters). MUS-Repair, which feeds minimal unsatisfiable subsets back to the generator, is strongest in every setting (+1.8 to +15.0 pp over the best non-MUS baseline). But the central finding is what repair leaves behind. After structured feedback, models rarely contradict themselves. They forget. Residual errors are 98-100% satisfiable drift across all settings, while contradiction drops to near zero. Reliable multi-turn systems must separately validate that the returned answer respects the maintained state. Code is available at https://github.com/kaons-research/drift-bench.

</details>


### [75] [MEMOR-E: In-Context and Fine-Tuned LLM Personalization for Alzheimer's Assistive Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23941)
*Maissa Abir Smaili,Eren Sadikoglu,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMOR-E，一种集成平板界面的四足机器人，通过微调大语言模型模拟阿尔茨海默病患者的认知行为，实现个性化辅助交互和可解释的认知评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病导致患者记忆和语言能力渐进性衰退，需要社交辅助机器人提供支持，但目前缺乏能根据患者认知阶段自适应调整的交互系统。

Method: 利用235名患者的音频转录和合成健康对照数据微调LLM以模拟阶段一致的认知行为；采用上下文学习让第二个LLM生成领域和严重级别的认知错误摘要；集成可解释AI机制。

Result: MEMOR-E能够生成阶段感知的非诊断性认知摘要，支持个性化辅助交互；可解释AI将模型输出转化为透明、人类可读的证据，实现可信的人机交互。

Conclusion: 该机器人系统通过LLM微调和ICL方法，在药物提醒、日常引导、记忆交互和陪伴等方面提供有效支持，同时确保照护者的监督和信任。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a neurodegenerative disorder marked by progressive declines in memory and language that reduce independence in daily life, motivating socially assistive robotic support. This paper presents MEMOR-E, a mobile quadruped robot with an interactive tablet interface that assists patients and caregivers through medication reminders, routine guidance, memory oriented interactions, and companionship. We evaluated the feasibility of fine tuning large language models (LLMs) to emulate stage consistent cognitive behavior and interpret responses across standard neuropsychological language tasks, using audio transcriptions from 235 Alzheimer's patients and synthetically generated healthy controls. We also report findings on using in context learning (ICL) in LLMs, where a second LLM produced domain and severity level cognitive error summaries. Our results show that MEMOR-E can generate stage aware, non diagnostic cognitive summaries that support personalized assistive interactions, while explainable AI mechanisms translate model outputs into transparent, human readable evidence to enable caregiver oversight and trustworthy human robot interaction.

</details>


### [76] [A Dynamical Framework for Cognitive Processes Based on Transformations and Semantic Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23942)
*Carlo Cattani,Dioneia Motta Monte-Serrat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从控制论视角提出了一个认知过程的结构与动力学框架，通过状态空间迭代更新规则 \(X_{t+1} = \pi(F(f(X_t)))\) 建模认知，并引入范畴论描述组合结构，通过不动点与压缩条件分析稳定性，最后通过计算示例和语言应用验证了框架的可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知模型缺乏统一的结构与动力学整合框架，难以在控制论视角下同时刻画认知状态的变换、解释与稳定化过程。

Method: 提出迭代更新规则 \(X_{t+1} = \pi(F(f(X_t)))\)，包含内部变换 \(f\)、解释映射 \(F\) 和语义等价约束 \(\pi\)；引入范畴论形式化组合结构；通过不动点定理和压缩映射条件分析动力学稳定性。

Result: 提供了计算示例验证框架的操作性；通过定性分析揭示了诱导动力学的行为特征；在语言应用中展示了上下文依赖解释如何收敛到稳定的语义类别。

Conclusion: 该框架将动力系统、范畴论与认知建模有机结合，为认知过程提供了一种统一表示——即认知是由反馈驱动、趋向不变解释的演化过程。

Abstract: This paper proposes a structural and dynamical framework for modeling cognitive processes within a cybernetic perspective. Cognitive states are represented as elements of a state space evolving through an iterative update rule of the form
  \[
  X_{t+1} = π\big(F(f(X_t))\big),
  \]
  where $f$ describes internal transformations, $F$ represents interpretative mappings, and $π$ enforces semantic equivalence.
  The model is interpreted as a feedback system integrating transformation, observation, and stabilization. A categorical formulation is introduced to capture compositional structure, while the associated dynamics are analyzed through fixed-point arguments and contraction conditions ensuring stability.
  To demonstrate the operational character of the framework, a computational illustration is provided, together with a qualitative analysis of the induced dynamics. A concrete linguistic application shows how context-dependent interpretation can be modeled as a trajectory toward a stable semantic class.
  The proposed approach connects dynamical systems, category theory, and cognitive modeling, and provides a unified representation of cognition as a feedback-driven process evolving toward invariant interpretations.

</details>


### [77] [Spacetime Formation under Requirements: Contextual Realization and Form-Dependent Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23943)
*Song-Ju Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出量子概率并非基础性的，而是有限状态要求在固定时空中无法满足时产生的投影效应，并据此构建了一套超越-操作实在论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子认知研究往往用固定事件结构上的量子概率替代经典概率，但未解释为何量子概率会出现在认知系统中。本文试图从有限状态表征等基础要求出发，推导量子概率的出现根源。

Method: 从有限表征容量、单态语义稳定性、上下文敏感干预等要求出发，论证当这些要求在单一全局布尔事件结构中无法实现时，固定时空投影会表现为非对易性、干涉和类量子概率。通过将经典上下文记账成本重新解释为上下文时空形成的固定时空投影，并引入类似和乐（holonomy）的概念来描述局部布尔逻辑世界之间的不匹配。

Result: 提出了一个超越-操作实在论解释：对象性、事件性、概率和时空均被视为在要求下的实现形式，而客观性由跨观察者、跨历史时空形成中保持的不变量定义。

Conclusion: 量子概率是经典系统在有限状态要求下，从上下文时空形成向固定时空投影时产生的现象，而非基础性的概率法则。这为认知量子效应提供了非神秘化的实在论解释。

Abstract: Quantum cognition often explains order effects, contextuality, and violations of the law of total probability by replacing classical probability with quantum probability on a fixed event structure. This paper proposes a different interpretation: quantum probability is the fixed-spacetime projection of contextual spacetime formation under finite-state requirements.
  The framework begins not with time, space, objects, or probabilities, but with requirements such as finite representational capacity, single-state semantic stability, context-sensitive intervention, avoidance of explicit context labels, coherent world-formation, and intersubjective transformability.
