We propose Market Regime Council (MRC), a cooperative multi-agent decision system that computes exact Shapley credits across all single, pairwise, and Grand-coalition outputs for online agent weighting. Instantiated with N=3 specialist agents, at each trading period, MRC recomputes coalition-based Shapley weights from exponentially weighted performance histories, uses a Bayesian adaptive mixture to stabilize early periods, applies regime-dependent multipliers to adjust agent authority, and records each rebalance through a five-layer causal trace. Over 1,037 trading days across 13 crypto assets and five seeds, MRC achieves a Sharpe ratio of 1.51 and a cumulative return of 440.1%, ranking first on CR, SR, and IR among active baselines and attaining the lowest MDD among active methods. Ablation results show that the gains come from Shapley-weighted integration across coalition outputs rather than from any single stage in isolation. Code and demo data are included in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [113] [Reasoning as an Attack Surface: Adaptive Evolutionary CoT Jailbreaks for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24497)
*Jianan Li,Simeng Qin,Xiaojun Jia,Lionel Z. Wang,Tianhang Zheng,Xiaoshuang Jia,Yang Liu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自适应进化思维链越狱框架AE-CoT，通过教师角色扮演、片段级交叉变异和自适应突变率控制，生成多样化有害CoT模板，有效突破大推理模型的安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型的显式思维链机制存在安全漏洞，现有静态CoT模板越狱方法缺乏多样性、适应性和有效性。

Method: 1) 将有害目标重写为温和提示并进行语义分解构建CoT候选池；2) 在结构化表示空间中进行多代进化搜索，采用片段级交叉和自适应突变率控制的变异策略；3) 独立评分模型进行分级评估，高分候选结合有害CoT模板增强攻击能力。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上，AE-CoT持续优于现有最先进的越狱方法。

Conclusion: 自适应进化CoT框架有效解决了静态模板的局限性，显著提升了越狱攻击的成功率和适应性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in reasoning and generation tasks and are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. However, their explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) mechanism introduces new security risks, making them particularly vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Existing approaches often rely on static CoT templates to elicit harmful outputs, but such fixed designs suffer from limited diversity, adaptability, and effectiveness. To overcome these limitations, we propose an adaptive evolutionary CoT jailbreak framework, called AE-CoT. Specifically, the method first rewrites harmful goals into mild prompts with teacher role-play and decomposes them into semantically coherent reasoning fragments to construct a pool of CoT jailbreak candidates. Then, within a structured representation space, we perform multi-generation evolutionary search, where candidate diversity is expanded through fragment-level crossover and a mutation strategy with an adaptive mutation-rate control mechanism. An independent scoring model provides graded harmfulness evaluations, and high-scoring candidates are further enhanced with a harmful CoT template to induce more destructive generations. Extensive experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed AE-CoT, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art jailbreak methods.

</details>


### [114] [Hypothesis Generation and Inductive Inference in Children and Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24528)
*Jeffrey Qin,Wasu Top Piriyakulki,Zhuangfei Gao,Mia Radovanovic,Jessica Sommerville,Kevin Ellis,Marta Kryven*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过归纳推理的Box任务，比较了人类儿童和LLM智能体在不确定环境下推断潜在因果的能力。研究发现，儿童的行为可通过主观证据可靠性和在线假设生成来解释，而LLM智能体在多项行为上复现了儿童模式，但在过度观察和过度服从指令方面存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类（儿童）和LLM智能体在不确定环境下进行因果推断的计算原则，以及两者是否表现出相似的行为模式。

Method: 采用归纳推理Box任务，将任务形式化为贝叶斯粒子滤波的程序归纳过程，通过约束满足和程序综合两种互补视角进行建模，对比分析儿童与LLM智能体的行为数据。

Result: 儿童行为可由主观证据可靠性和在线假设生成解释；LLM智能体复制了儿童对证据可靠性和可观测性变化的响应模式，但表现出过度观察和过度服从指令的倾向。

Conclusion: 儿童和LLM智能体对环境结构的适应方式相似，但在信息寻求行为上存在不同的内在成本和归纳偏置。

Abstract: Real world decision-making requires constructing mental models under uncertainty over evidence, over the underlying causal rules, and over the state of the world itself. Which computational principles underpin human inference under such conditions, and do LLM-based agents exhibit similar behavior given matching constraints? We address these questions using an inductive inference Box Task in which participants, human children and LLM-based agents, infer a latent cause through sequential interaction with an uncertain environment. We formalize this task as program induction with Bayesian particle-based inference, admitting two complementary interpretations: (1) as a constraint satisfaction process over hypotheses, and (2) as a program synthesis problem in which hypotheses are executable programs evaluated against evidence. Using the constraint-based formulation, we show that children's behavior is best explained by a combination of subjective evidence reliability and online hypothesis generation, accounting for both their evidence-seeking patterns and their dissociation between task completion and rule generalization. Using the program synthesis formulation, we treat LLM-based agents as model organisms: controllable systems that allow systematic manipulation of task conditions. Across backends, LLM-based agents replicate children's responses to changes in evidence reliability and observability, including discounting unreliable evidence, seeking to resolve partial information, and dissociating between task completion and causal generalization. At the same time, LLM-based agents tend to over-observe and over-comply with instructions relative to children. These results suggest that while children and LLM-based agents adapt similarly to environmental structure, their information-seeking behavior exhibits distinct underlying costs and inductive biases.

</details>


### [115] [DemoEvolve: Overcoming Sparse Feedback in Agentic Harness Evolution with Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24539)
*Lirong Che,Yuzhe yang,Peiwen lin,Chuang wang,Xueqian wang,Jian su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DemoEvolve是一种基于示范引导的智能体框架进化方法，通过人类专家轨迹替代稀疏奖励信号，解决长时域随机环境中自生成轨迹进化不稳定的问题，在Liar's Dice和Balatro任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域随机环境中，自生成轨迹（self-rollout）的奖励稀疏、结果方差大、失败难以归因到具体框架机制，导致智能体通过自我练习获取任务能力的范式变得脆弱。

Method: 提出DemoEvolve方法，利用人类专家示范轨迹作为编码提议者的参考经验，引导框架层的诊断和编辑，替代纯奖励驱动的搜索方式。

Result: 在Liar's Dice中，短回合、可归因失败场景下自进化有效；在Balatro（长时域随机环境）中，自进化因稀疏反馈和候选选择噪声而失败，DemoEvolve在相同预算下产生更有效、可审计的框架编辑并取得更好性能。

Conclusion: 示范引导使稀疏反馈下的框架进化更可诊断、可定位、更稳定。

Abstract: Agent harness evolution improves frozen language-model agents by modifying the executable structures around them. We study this paradigm as a form of sample-efficient fast adaptation: instead of updating model weights, an agent can acquire task-specific competence by changing its external harness, while leaving the base model's general capabilities intact. Prior work shows that self-generated rollouts can support harness search, suggesting that agents may acquire new task competence through practice. Yet in long-horizon stochastic environments, self-practice becomes fragile: rewards are sparse, outcomes are high-variance, and failures are hard to attribute to concrete harness mechanisms. We introduce DemoEvolve, a demonstration-bootstrapped approach to harness evolution. When reward-only search is too broad and noisy, competent human trajectories serve as expert reference experience for the coding proposer, guiding harness-level diagnosis and editing. Experiments on Liar's Dice show that self-rollout evolution can work when episodes are short and failures are attributable. In contrast, Balatro exposes a harder long-horizon stochastic regime, where self-rollout evolution is misled by sparse feedback and candidate-selection noise, while tutorial-like textual knowledge alone does not yield stable improvement. Under the same limited budget, DemoEvolve produces more effective and auditable harness edits and achieves better performance. Overall, demonstrations make sparse-feedback harness evolution more diagnosable, localizable, and stable.

</details>


### [116] [Beyond Control-Flow: Integrating the Resource Perspective into Multi-Collaborative Process Modeling from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24546)
*Anton Antonov,Humam Kourani,Alessandro Berti,Gyunam Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种资源感知的生成管道，能从自然语言描述中自动生成包含泳池和泳道的BPMN 2.0协作图，在保持控制流质量的同时实现了资源发现，且运行时开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到模型方法主要关注控制流视角（活动排序），忽略了业务流程中的协作方面（如组织角色和跨组织交互），缺乏对资源（泳池/泳道）的建模支持。

Method: 设计了一个紧凑、可执行的中间语言，强制要求资源细节（组织对应泳池，角色对应泳道）；使用消息事件表示跨组织依赖；通过正交布局例程自动处理元素空间排列；结合多种LLM进行实验。

Result: 在10个业务流程和9个LLM上的实验表明，该方法在资源发现方面表现强劲，同时保持了控制流质量，仅带来边际运行时开销。

Conclusion: 该资源感知生成管道推动了生成式建模向更全面、多协作的业务操作表示方向发展。

Abstract: Process modeling is a sub-domain of Business Process Management (BPM) focused on the translation of process artifacts into formal models. This task traditionally requires extensive human input and domain expertise in both BPM notations and the specific business context. While Large Language Models (LLMs) can now automate much of this manual work, current text-to-model approaches focus predominantly on the control-flow perspective-ordering activities without considering the collaborative aspect of the processes. In this paper, we introduce a resource-aware generation pipeline that produces formal BPMN 2.0 collaboration diagrams from natural-language descriptions. Rather than solely prompting an LLM for raw XML, we describe a compact, executable intermediate language with mandatory resource details defining both the organization (pool) and the role (lane). Cross-organization dependencies are materialized using the standard formal notation for such interactions-message events-while an orthogonal layout routine automatically handles the spatial arrangement of elements within pools and lanes. Experiments on ten business processes with nine LLMs show strong resource discovery while preserving control-flow quality and adding only marginal runtime overhead. This approach moves generative modeling toward a more comprehensive, multi-collaborative representation of business operations.

</details>


### [117] [PALoRA: Projection-Adaptive LoRA for Preserving Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24549)
*Mustafa Hayri Bilgin,Mariam Barry,Albert Bifet,Azzedine Idir Ait Said,Soumya Banerjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型更新知识时损害已有推理能力的问题（塑性-稳定性困境），本文提出PALoRA两阶段框架：先通过SVF专家学习推理关键成分，再用正交约束的LoRA进行知识注入，避免干扰重要子空间。在Llama 3.1 8B和Mistral 7B上，该方法在保持95%推理性能的同时实现了高效知识注入，额外参数开销小于0.006%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在注入新知识时，参数高效微调方法会侵蚀已有的推理能力，这反映了塑性-稳定性困境。现有方法未能充分理解推理能力在模型参数中的分布机制。

Method: PALoRA两阶段框架：1) 先训练SVF专家，学习奇异缩放向量作为几何探针，识别推理关键成分；2) 在知识注入阶段采用LoRA并施加结构正交约束，确保更新避开已识别的技能相关子空间。

Result: 在Llama 3.1 8B和Mistral 7B上，在数学、代码和科学推理基准上，PALoRA平均保留SVF专家95%的推理性能，同时保持竞争力的知识召回，优于先前谱PEFT方法，额外参数开销小于0.006%。

Conclusion: 推理关键信息分布在奇异谱的多个方向上而非仅集中于主导方向，通过识别并保护这些子空间，可以在注入新知识时有效减少对已有推理能力的干扰，为LLM持续学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Efficiently updating Large Language Models (LLMs) with new or evolving factual knowledge remains a central challenge, as even parameter-efficient adaptation can erode previously acquired reasoning abilities. This tension reflects a plasticity-stability dilemma: models must incorporate new knowledge while preserving skill-critical representations. In this work, we study this trade-off through the spectral structure of multilayer perceptron weight matrices. We show, both theoretically and empirically, that information essential for reasoning is not localized only in dominant singular directions, but is instead distributed across the singular spectrum. Motivated by this observation, we introduce PALoRA, a two-stage framework for knowledge injection with reduced interference. PALoRA first trains a Singular Value Fine-Tuning (SVF) expert on a reasoning dataset and uses its learned singular scaling vector as a frozen geometric probe to identify components that are critical for the target skill. It then performs factual knowledge injection with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) under a structural orthogonality constraint, ensuring that updates avoid the identified skill-relevant subspace. Across Llama 3.1 8B and Mistral 7B, and across mathematical, coding, and scientific reasoning benchmarks, PALoRA preserves on average 95% of the SVF expert's reasoning performance while maintaining competitive factual recall. It consistently improves skill retention over prior spectral Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) methods while adding less than 0.006% parameter overhead.

</details>


### [118] [Jailbreak to Protect: Buffering and Reinforcing via Temporary Jailbreaking for Safe Fine-Tuning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24550)
*Seokil Ham,Jaehyuk Jang,Wonjun Lee,Changick Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Buffer-and-Reinforce微调框架，通过BufferLoRA临时越狱来缓冲有害更新，再通过ReinforceLoRA强化安全对齐，有效防御有害微调攻击，且无需额外安全数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FaaS服务中，有害微调攻击会削弱LLM的安全对齐。现有临时越狱防御方法机制尚不清晰，需要更深入理解其原理并设计更高效的防御框架。

Method: 提出Buffer-and-Reinforce框架：1）BufferLoRA作为可移除适配器诱导临时越狱，缓冲有害梯度；2）ReinforceLoRA在临时越狱状态下训练恢复拒绝行为；3）通过QR分解合并与UserLoRA集成，在保证用户任务性能的同时强化安全。

Result: 在有害微调攻击下，该框架在安全性和任务效用上均达到优异表现，且在用户微调过程中无需额外安全数据，计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 梯度层面分析表明临时越狱通过饱和安全退化梯度同时保留良性任务梯度的方式起作用。Buffer-and-Reinforce框架为FaaS场景下的安全防御提供了有效且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning-as-a-Service (FaaS) enables personalization of large language models (LLMs), but it can weaken safety-alignment under harmful fine-tuning attacks. Recent work has shown that activating harmful-behavior modules during fine-tuning can prevent models from learning undesired behaviors, but its mechanism remains unclear. In this paper, we revisit temporary jailbreaking as a defense against harmful fine-tuning and provide a gradient-level analysis showing that it saturates safety-degrading gradients while preserving benign task-relevant gradients. Based on this insight, we propose a Buffer-and-Reinforce fine-tuning framework that buffers harmful updates during user fine-tuning and reinforces safety after adaptation. Specifically, BufferLoRA induces temporary jailbreaking as a removable adapter to reduce harmful updates during user fine-tuning. After adaptation, ReinforceLoRA, trained to recover refusal behavior under the temporarily jailbroken state, is integrated with UserLoRA via QR decomposition-based merging to reinforce safety while preserving user-task performance. Extensive experiments show that our framework achieves superior safety and utility with no additional safety data during user fine-tuning and minimal computational cost.

</details>


### [119] [Summoning the Oracle to Slay It: Mitigating Look-Ahead Bias in Financial Backtesting with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24564)
*Weixian Waylon Li,Mengyu Wang,Tiejun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinCAD方法，通过推理时上下文感知解码抑制大语言模型对历史金融数据的记忆偏差，无需重训即可提升回测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在历史金融数据上回测不可靠，因为预训练数据截止时间晚于历史事件发生时间，模型已"知道"历史结果，导致参数化的前瞻性偏差。

Method: 提出FinCAD方法，包含两个核心组件：1）对抗性偏差发现流程，学习模型特定的记忆激活先验提示；2）实体与日期自适应规则，根据每个（实体，日期）的记忆程度动态调整上下文感知解码强度，惩罚记忆的样本内日期，在样本外衰减为零。

Result: 在5个7-14B参数LLM和5只大型股票上，FinCAD在记忆日期上将样本内回测收益降低最多67.1%，同时2025年样本外收益偏差在$8K以内，夏普比率偏差在0.10以内，通用推理能力保持1.7分以内。在11个模型的排行榜上，将样本内/样本外斯皮尔曼相关系数从0.779提升至0.846。

Conclusion: FinCAD有效抑制了LLM在金融回测中的历史记忆偏差，无需重训即可获得更真实反映模型样本外表现的评估结果，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Backtesting large language models (LLMs) on historical financial data is unreliable because pre-training cuts off after the events happened. An LLM trained in 2024 already "knows" which way 2018-2020 stocks moved. We name this failure parametric look-ahead bias and propose FinCAD, an inference-time adaptation of Context-Aware Decoding that suppresses an LLM's memory of historical outcomes without retraining. FinCAD pairs an adversarial bias-discovery pipeline that learns a model-specific memory-activating prior prompt with an entity- and date-adaptive rule that scales the CAD strength to per-(entity, date) memorisation, so the penalty fires on memorised in-sample dates and decays to zero out-of-sample. Across five 7-14B LLMs and five mega-cap equities, FinCAD cuts in-sample backtest returns by up to -67.1% on memorised dates while leaving 2025 out-of-sample returns within $8K and Sharpe within 0.10 of baseline, and preserves general-purpose reasoning within 1.7 pts. On an eleven-model leaderboard, it raises the in-sample / out-of-sample Spearman correlation from +0.779 to +0.846, recovering rankings that genuinely predict out-of-sample performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Algometrics: Forecasting Under Algorithmic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23978)
*Marc Schmitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"算法计量学"（algometrics）框架，研究在算法市场中，预测模型影响其预测对象（时间序列）的反馈效应。区分了历史风险（被动预测）与部署风险（预测驱动行动），并证明部署风险无法仅从被动历史数据识别，历史模型排名在拥挤环境下可能反转，且随机化或工具化行动可识别短期线性反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算法市场中，预测模型一旦被用于交易、配置、执行或风险控制，会改变其试图预测的未来数据分布。现有时间序列评估方法忽略这一反馈效应，导致部署风险被低估，因此需要新框架量化这种"自反性"风险。

Method: 提出algometrics框架，通过区分被动预测（不改变数据生成过程）与主动部署（预测驱动行动改变数据）来建模。利用线性反馈模型、历史模型排名反转分析以及有限样本界的推导，证明部署风险不可识别性、排名反转现象，并给出识别短期反馈的可行方案。

Result: 三个核心结果：1) 部署风险无法仅从被动历史数据唯一识别；2) 历史模型排名在算法拥挤时可能完全反转；3) 随机化或工具化行动可在有限样本下一致估计部署风险。

Conclusion: 算法市场中的时间序列基准应同时报告预测精度和反馈敏感性，以更全面评估模型的真实部署风险。

Abstract: In algorithmic markets, predictive models become part of the data-generating process they aim to forecast. Once their outputs are converted into trades, allocations, execution schedules, or risk controls, they change the future data on which they are evaluated. I introduce algometrics, a framework for time series whose evolution depends on the predictive algorithms forecasting them. The framework distinguishes historical risk, measured under passive forecasting, from deployment risk, measured when forecasts drive actions. I prove three results. First, deployment risk is not identifiable from passive historical data alone: even in a one-step linear feedback model, infinitely many algorithm-mediated environments induce the same historical law while implying different deployment risks for the same forecaster. Second, historical model rankings can invert under crowding, so a predictor with lower passive error can have higher deployment error once similar algorithms are adopted. Third, randomized or instrumented actions identify short-horizon linear feedback, and I derive a finite-sample bound for deployment-risk estimation. These results suggest that time-series benchmarks in algorithmic markets should report feedback sensitivity alongside predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [121] [Iterative Refinement Neural Operators are Learned Fixed-Point Solvers: A Principled Approach to Spectral Bias Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24041)
*Xiaotian Liu,Shuyuan Shang,Xiaopeng Wang,Pu Ren,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代细化神经算子（IRNO），通过将预训练算子与固定点迭代的细化模块结合，有效克服神经算子的频谱偏差，显著提升高频细节的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经算子采用单次推理，难以捕捉高频细节（频谱偏差），限制了其在复杂物理系统建模中的精度。

Method: IRNO在预训练算子基础上引入可学习的细化模块，通过固定点迭代逐步修正残差；同时提出渐进式频谱损失，在训练过程中逐阶段增加对高频分量的惩罚。

Result: 在湍流模拟中误差降低56.05%；在活性物质系统中，低、中、高频归一化误差比分别降至27.72-36.10%、5.07-6.68%、1.48-2.04%，且超越训练迭代次数后仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: IRNO通过迭代细化策略有效缓解频谱偏差，为神经算子在高精度科学计算中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neural operators serve as fast, data-driven surrogates for scientific modeling but typically rely on a monolithic, single-pass inference procedure that struggles to resolve high-frequency details, a limitation known as spectral bias. We introduce the Iterative Refinement Neural Operator (IRNO), which augments pre-trained operators with a learned refinement module iteratively applied via fixed-point iteration. IRNO decomposes the prediction into a coarse initialization followed by successive residual corrections, paralleling classical numerical solvers. Under local assumptions, we establish contraction of the induced operator, ensuring convergence to a unique fixed point. To explicitly target high-frequency errors, we propose a progressive spectral loss that adaptively increases penalty on high-frequency components over refinement steps during training. Across physical systems, IRNO consistently lowers error, with up to 56.05% improvement on turbulent flow. On Active Matter, spectral analysis reveals that, relative to base operator, the normalized error ratios decrease to 27.72-36.10% in low-, 5.07-6.68% in mid-, and 1.48-2.04% in high-frequencies, remaining stable beyond the trained iteration count. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaotianliu-dartmouth/Iterative_Refinement_Neural_Operator

</details>


### [122] [Hidden-State Privacy Has an Empty Middle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24042)
*Alexander Okezue Bell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯发布下的单层隐藏状态隐私问题，发现没有机制能在中等效用和中等隐私之间取得平衡。作者提出了Fisher球下界理论，证明了对角逆Fisher发布是最优对角机制，但处于隐私/效用边缘而非中间区域。实验表明，通用特征值机制在自适应攻击下完全失效，而拆分记忆Transformer在隐私保护上显著优于同等预算的GPT基线。这些结果将隐藏状态发布问题从高斯类内的机制设计转向架构或发布协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯发布机制在隐藏状态隐私保护中无法同时实现中等效用和中等隐私，存在"空中间"现象，即没有机制能在两者之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 通过对1536个高斯发布协方差进行实证测试，提出Fisher球下界理论证明；设计对角逆Fisher发布机制；提出拆分记忆Transformer架构进行实验验证。

Result: 对角逆Fisher发布是最优对角机制但处于隐私/效用边缘；通用特征值机制在自适应Mahalanobis攻击下完全失效（100% top-1）；拆分记忆Transformer在90M参数时G_Mah达到[20,33]，比同预算GPT基线有6-24倍优势。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态发布问题需要从高斯类内的机制设计转向架构或发布协同设计。

Abstract: Of $1{,}536$ Gaussian release covariances we tested for single-layer hidden-state privacy, zero achieve both moderate utility and moderate privacy against an adaptive retrieval attacker. We prove a complementary Fisher-ball lower bound: every full-rank Gaussian release at $O(1)$ Fisher utility admits a direction whose Mahalanobis signal grows linearly in hidden width, ruling out uniform Gaussian safety in the class and matching the empirical empty middle. The diagonal inverse-Fisher release $Σ^\star_{\mathrm{diag}}(\mathcal{K}) = (2\mathcal{K}/d)\,\mathrm{diag}(1/F_{ii})$ is the unique minimax-optimal diagonal mechanism at first-order KL budget $\mathcal{K}$ and the only release with worst-attacker top-1 $\le 0.001$ at every point of a 32 model-layer grid, but it sits on a privacy/utility edge rather than filling the middle. A generalized-eigen mechanism reaching $13\times$ Pareto reduction under Euclidean retrieval collapses to $100\%$ top-1 under the adaptive Mahalanobis attacker, and a full-trajectory sequence inverter recovers $94\%$ of clean GPT-2 prefixes but $0\%$ under $Σ_{\mathrm{diag}}$. A split-memory transformer trained from scratch reaches $G_{\mathrm{Mah}} \in [20, 33]$ at 90M and maintains a $6$--$24\times$ advantage over same-budget GPT baselines from 30M to 1B at a fixed-token language-modeling loss penalty; pretrained models top out at 9.3. These results reframe hidden-state release from mechanism-design within the Gaussian class to architecture or release co-design.

</details>


### [123] [A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark: Do Protein-Ligand Models Learn Binding Sites or Just Binding Likelihood?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24045)
*Zhaohan Meng,Zhen Bai,Ke Yuan,Iadh Ounis,Zaiqiao Meng,Hao Xu,Joseph Loscalzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InteractBind是一个大规模蛋白质-配体数据集（约10万对），提供了结合位点定位等细粒度评估基准，发现现有模型在二元结合预测上表现良好但结合位点定位能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质-配体基准仅评估二元结合预测和亲和力回归，无法检验模型是否能够定位结合位点或识别分子识别背后的非共价相互作用。

Method: 构建包含约10万蛋白质-配体对的数据集，涵盖六种非共价相互作用的残基-原子交互图，设计结合位点定位任务，并引入亲和力和蛋白质相似性控制的数据划分。

Result: 评估8种现有模型，发现模型在二元结合预测上表现良好，但结合位点定位能力有限，且不同非共价相互作用类型间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: InteractBind建立了一种新的评估范式，鼓励开发更具可解释性和物理基础的蛋白质-配体模型。

Abstract: Protein-ligand modeling underpins computational drug discovery and molecular design. Existing protein-ligand benchmarks typically evaluate whether a protein and ligand interact and how strongly they bind, through tasks such as binary binding prediction and affinity regression. However, these evaluations provide limited evidence of whether models can localize binding sites or identify the non-covalent interactions underlying molecular recognition. To address this gap, we introduce InteractBind, a large-scale protein-ligand dataset comprising approximately 100k protein-ligand pairs, together with a benchmark for fine-grained evaluation. The core fine-grained task is that of binding-site localization, which uses protein-residue and ligand-atom interaction maps spanning six major types of non-covalent interactions to assess whether model-derived interaction maps localize binding sites. InteractBind further includes binding affinity and protein similarity-controlled splits to support realistic generalization assessment. Using InteractBind, we evaluate eight existing sequence-based and interaction-aware models, assessing binary binding prediction and binding-site localization. Results reveal limited binding-site localization despite strong binary binding prediction, with marked variation across non-covalent interaction types. Overall, InteractBind establishes a benchmark paradigm that encourages the development of more interpretable and physically grounded protein-ligand models.

</details>


### [124] [Mixture of Complementary Agents for Robust LLM Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24048)
*Yichi Zhang,Kevin Lu,Yuang Zhang,Jie Gao,Lirong Xia,Fang-Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多AI协作中的提议者选择问题重新定义为类似特征选择的组合优化问题，强调模型间的互补性，并探索了计算可行的贪心选择算法来平衡性能与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在选择提议者时主要关注准确率或多样性，忽略了提议者之间以及提议者与汇总器之间的交互作用，导致选择效果不佳。

Method: 将提议者选择重构为组合选择问题，借鉴特征选择思想衡量模型互补性，并设计多种计算可行的贪心选择算法。

Result: 实验验证了互补性作为提议者选择指导原则的有效性，并找出了在实际中实现最佳性能-成本权衡的方法。

Conclusion: 互补性是多AI协作中选择提议者的关键原则，贪心算法能在计算可行范围内取得良好效果。

Abstract: Multi-AI collaboration, such as ensembling or debating large language models (LLMs), is a promising paradigm for aggregating information and boosting performance. A foundational step in these pipelines is to feed the responses of several proposer LLMs into a summarizer LLM, which synthesizes a better answer. However, choosing which proposers to include is non-trivial. Existing approaches primarily focus either on accuracy (picking the strongest models) or diversity (ensuring variety), and often overlook the interactions among proposers and with the summarizer. We reframe proposer selection as a combinatorial selection problem akin to feature selection, where the value of an LLM lies in its complementarity with others. However, directly applying standard feature-selection algorithms is impractical in the LLM setting due to prohibitive time complexity. Motivated by this limitation, we explore an extensive range of computationally feasible, greedy-style selection algorithms that assess complementarity using a small labeled set. Our experiments validate complementarity as a guiding principle for proposer selection and identify methods that achieve the best performance-cost trade-offs in practice.

</details>


### [125] [Truthful Online Preference Aggregation for LLM Fine-Tuning in Mobile Crowdsourcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24052)
*Shugang Hao,Lingjie Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向移动众包中LLM微调的在线真值偏好聚合机制，通过动态贝叶斯博弈建模和在线加权聚合，确保策略性工人真实反馈，实现次线性遗憾O(√T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动众包平台中，工人可能策略性地谎报偏好反馈以最大化自身影响力或收益，现有方法（如EM-based权重估计）无法在在线环境下识别最准确的工人，导致线性遗憾O(T)。

Method: 提出了一种新的动态贝叶斯博弈来建模平台与策略性移动工人之间的多智能体在线学习过程，并设计了在线加权聚合机制，根据工人反馈准确性动态调整其聚合权重。

Result: 机制确保策略性工人真实反馈，在标准场景下实现次线性遗憾O(√T)；在每时隙工人反馈受限的挑战场景下仍保证次线性遗憾O(√T)。真实数据集上的LLM微调实验显示该机制显著优于基准方案。

Conclusion: 该工作为移动众包中LLM与人类反馈对齐提供了真值激励的理论框架和实用方案，有效解决了在线环境下的策略性谎报问题。

Abstract: To better serve users' demands in mobile applications (e.g., navigation), mobile crowdsourcing platforms can iteratively align large language model (LLM)-generated content (e.g., AI-generated traffic condition predictions) with human feedback collected from crowdsourcing workers (e.g., mobile users). However, workers may strategically misreport their online preference feedback to maximize their influence or payment. Existing pipelines in mobile crowdsourcing (e.g., EM-based weight estimation) fail to identify the most accurate worker in this online setting, resulting in a linear regret $\mathcal{O}(T)$ over $T$ time slots. In this paper, we study truthful online preference aggregation for LLM fine-tuning in mobile crowdsourcing. We formulate a new dynamic Bayesian game to model the multi-agent online learning process between the platform and strategic mobile workers. We propose a novel online weighted aggregation mechanism that dynamically adjusts each worker's weight in the preference aggregation according to their feedback accuracy. We prove that our mechanism ensures truthful feedback from strategic workers and achieves a sublinear regret $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ over $T$ time slots. We further extend our mechanism to a challenging scenario with limited worker feedback per time slot, still guaranteeing a sublinear regret $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$. Experiments on LLM fine-tuning with real-world datasets further demonstrate significant performance gains of our mechanisms over benchmark schemes.

</details>


### [126] [Cascade-KDE: Robust Time-Series Restoration under Out-of-Distribution Impulse Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24055)
*Yuefeng Liu,Ning Yang,Ziyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cascade-KDE，一种基于密度估计的无训练时间序列修复方法，能在去除高斯噪声和脉冲异常值的同时保留局部结构特征，在多个基准上优于传统滤波器和深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实时间序列常被高斯噪声和大尺度脉冲异常混合污染，现有方法在去除异常时容易损坏导数峰值等任务关键特征，缺乏同时保证低重建误差和局部结构保留的鲁棒修复方案。

Method: 先估计二维时间-幅度密度，再用密度截断鲁棒期望来限制远距离异常点的影响，最后通过指数级联与自适应停止来精细修复序列。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，Cascade-KDE在曲线保真度、导数保留、下游分类和运行效率方面一致优于经典滤波器和代表性学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于有界密度的修复方法是噪声时间序列流水线中一种实用的特征保留预处理选择。

Abstract: Real-world time-series data in industrial sensing, healthcare, and energy systems is often corrupted by a mixture of Gaussian noise and occasional large-magnitude impulse outliers. For tasks that depend on local shape, such as ECG morphology analysis and battery degradation monitoring, the main requirement is not only low reconstruction error but also preservation of derivative peaks and task-critical features. We propose Cascade-KDE, a training-free restoration framework for corrupted time series. The method first estimates a two-dimensional temporal-amplitude density, then applies a Density-Truncated Robust Expectation to limit the influence of distant abnormal points, and finally refines the sequence through an exponential cascade with adaptive stopping. This design aims to improve robustness under out-of-distribution impulse corruptions while keeping the restored trajectory close to the original local structure. Across several benchmark datasets, the proposed method shows consistent gains over classical filters and representative learning-based baselines on curve fidelity, derivative preservation, downstream classification, and runtime efficiency. These results suggest that bounded density-based restoration is a practical option for feature-preserving preprocessing in noisy time-series pipelines.

</details>


### [127] [Signs Beat Floats: Low-Rank Double-Binary Adaptation for On-Device Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24058)
*Yoshihiko Fujisawa,Yuma Ichikawa,Yudai Fujimoto,Akira Sakai,Katsuki Fujisawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRDBA，一种将LoRA适配器的低秩因子替换为二进制符号载体的方法，通过通道级缩放表示幅度，在保持适配器模式推理效率的同时大幅压缩模型体积，并在某些场景下匹配fp16 LoRA的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有设备端大语言模型适配通常采用量化基模型+LoRA适配器的方案，但未合并的适配器模式中LoRA仍引入了密集浮点分支，增加了存储和计算开销。作者希望进一步压缩适配器体积，同时保持推理效率和性能。

Method: LoRDBA将LoRA的两个低秩因子替换为二进制符号矩阵（仅含±1），并通过轻量级通道级缩放向量来表示幅度。这使密集适配器分支转化为两个符号累加矩阵乘法与通道级缩放交替进行的操作。

Result: 在适配器模式实验中，LoRDBA在相同模型大小下优于低比特基线，在特定场景下匹配fp16 LoRA质量。未合并适配器在秩r=16时预填充延迟开销最多8%，而适配器体积减少超过10倍；训练内存开销约为fp16 LoRA的1.6倍。

Conclusion: LoRDBA通过二值化LoRA因子实现了极致的适配器压缩，在保证推理性能和效率的同时显著降低存储需求，适合设备端部署场景。

Abstract: On-device adaptation of large language models commonly keeps a quantized base model frozen while training and deploying a small, task-specific LoRA adapter. In the unmerged adapter-mode setting, however, the adapter is more than a compact storage module; it introduces an additional dense floating-point branch, maintains a trainable state for local updates, and acts as a unit of communication and hot-swapping.We introduce LoRDBA, a LoRA-compatible adapter that replaces both low-rank factors with binary sign carriers while representing magnitudes through lightweight, channel-wise scales, converting the dense adapter branch into two sign-accumulation matrix multiplications interleaved with channel-wise scaling. A finite-sample analysis shows that reconstruction quality is governed by the residual-to-magnitude ratio of the original LoRA factors. In adapter-mode experiments, LoRDBA outperforms low-bit baselines at matched model sizes while matching fp16 LoRA quality in selected regimes. The unmerged adapter incurs at most 8% prefill latency overhead at matched rank r=16 despite an over 10x reduction in adapter footprint, with moderate training memory overhead of approximately 1.6x that of fp16 LoRA.

</details>


### [128] [Spectral Probe-Circuits: A Three-Step Recipe for Identifying Attention-Head Circuits in Pretrained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24059)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种三步法来识别预训练Transformer中的注意力头电路：基于谱信号的头排序、任务模式筛选、分组消融验证。实验证明该方法跨参数规模、架构和预训练流程均有效，且无需监督信号即可发现因果电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖标签或归因梯度来识别Transformer中的注意力头电路，缺乏无监督且跨模型通用的高效识别手段。

Method: 三步法：(1) 计算每个注意力头输出的时间积分参与比作为谱信号，对头部进行排序；(2) 通过任务模式筛选将通用指标转化为任务特定的候选电路；(3) 使用分组消融与匹配随机对照组进行因果验证。

Result: 在51M至1B活跃/7B总参数量范围内、两种架构（密集、混合专家）和四种预训练流程上验证有效；消融后合成归纳任务top-1下降94-100%；归纳电路保持在3-11个头（亚线性于总头数）；可识别专用计算的头比例稳定在17-19%。

Conclusion: 该无监督方法能可靠识别不同规模和架构下Transformer中的因果注意力电路，且电路大小随模型扩展呈亚线性增长，为理解Transformer内部机制提供了通用方法论。

Abstract: We present a three-step recipe for identifying attention-head circuits in pretrained transformers. A per-head spectral signal -- the time-integrated participation ratio of each head's attention output -- ranks heads doing sustained content-dependent computation without labels or attribution gradients. A task-pattern screen filters this general indicator into a task-specific candidate circuit, and group ablation against a matched-random control completes the causal claim. We validate across an 8x parameter range (51M to 1B-active / 7B-total), two architecture families (dense, mixture-of-experts), and four pretraining pipelines. The recipe ports: a 2-6 head induction circuit is causally necessary in every model tested, with a 94-100% drop in synthetic-induction top-1 after ablation. The spectral signal is predictive without supervision: on six independent seeds of a 51M-parameter probe model, the same computation identifies the seed-specific circuit on each seed. The fraction of heads doing identifiable specialized computation is conserved at 17-19% across the Pythia family (124M to 410M), while specific induction circuits stay 3-11 heads -- sublinear in total head count. This paper is the methodology anchor of a three-paper program; companion papers extend the recipe to developmental trajectories during pretraining and to composed-task circuits where pattern selectivity decouples from task-causal structure.

</details>


### [129] [Federated Learning over Human-Body Communication for On-Body Edge Intelligence: A Survey, Taxonomy, and BODYFED-HBC Scheduling Vignette](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24062)
*Koffka Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了人体通信（HBC）与联邦学习（FL）在可穿戴体域网中的交叉研究，提出了区分四种FL部署场景的分类法，并指出“身体信道感知FL”这一开放问题。同时介绍了BODYFED-HBC参考架构、优化公式和调度算法，以及结合公开数据集与经验信号损耗模型的可复现仿真方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HBC与FL在可穿戴设备领域各自具有优势，但两者研究目前联系薄弱：HBC研究忽略了学习与模型更新流量，而FL研究通常抽象化了通信层。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，探索两者的交叉融合。

Method: 通过综述现有文献，提出区分体内、体中心、跨用户和临床云FL部署的分类法；定义“身体信道感知FL”问题；设计BODYFED-HBC参考架构、优化公式和调度算法；并构建结合公开数据集与经验人体耦合通信信号损耗模型的仿真框架。

Result: 提出了身体信道感知FL的研究议程，明确了HBC链路、剩余能量、传感器内存和隐私风险对FL客户端选择、更新压缩和聚合的影响；给出了可复现的仿真方案和开放的研究方向。

Conclusion: HBC与FL的深度融合是提升可穿戴体域网通信效率与隐私保护的关键路径，未来需关注身体信道感知的协议设计、开放数据集构建及跨层优化。

Abstract: Human-body communication (HBC) is a promising physical substrate for wearable body-area networks because it can localize communication around the body and reduce the burden of conventional radio links. Federated learning (FL) is a promising learning substrate because it can reduce raw-data centralization for physiological and behavioral sensing. Yet these two literatures remain weakly connected: FL for wearables usually abstracts the communication layer, whereas HBC research usually abstracts learning and model-update traffic. This article surveys the intersection of HBC, wireless body-area networks, wearable FL, Internet-of-Bodies privacy, and edge-intelligence optimization. We propose a taxonomy that distinguishes intra-body, body-hub, cross-user, and clinical-cloud FL deployments, and we identify the open problem of body-channel-aware FL: learning protocols whose client selection, update compression, and aggregation are controlled by posture-dependent HBC links, residual energy, sensor memory, and privacy risk. To make the research agenda concrete, we introduce BODYFED-HBC as a reference architecture and provide an optimization formulation and scheduling algorithm. We further specify a reproducible simulation vignette that combines public wearable datasets with empirical body-coupled-communication signal-loss models. The article concludes with open datasets, evaluation metrics, limitations, and research directions for computer scientists working above the hardware layer.

</details>


### [130] [Generative Representation Learning on Hyper-relational Knowledge Graphs via Masked Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24064)
*Jaejun Lee,Seheon Kim,Joyce Jiyoung Whang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KREPE，首个基于掩码离散扩散的生成式表示学习方法，用于超关系知识图谱（HKG）的事实生成任务，能够处理任意缺失成分的查询，统一了链接预测与事实生成，并在基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HKG推理方法仅处理简单链接预测（假设事实中仅一个空白），无法应对现实场景中多个甚至全部成分同时缺失的情况。

Method: 提出KREPE，采用掩码离散扩散模型，通过上下文消息传递建模事实内部依赖，通过随机采样子图聚合建模事实间相关，统一了链接预测与事实生成。

Result: 在标准HKG链接预测基准上达到SOTA性能，且在新事实生成任务上优于基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: KREPE成功将HKG推理从单一的链接预测拓展到更通用的事实生成任务，验证了生成式方法在超关系知识图谱上的潜力。

Abstract: Hyper-relational knowledge graphs (HKGs) effectively represent complex facts. While inferring new knowledge in HKGs is a critical problem, current methods cast it as a simple link prediction, assuming that nearly all entities and relations within a fact are known, leaving only a single blank to be filled. However, this restricted assumption may not hold in real-world scenarios in which multiple, or even all, constituent components of a fact may be missing simultaneously. To bridge this gap, we introduce a task called fact generation: generating a valid hyper-relational fact from an arbitrarily masked query, i.e., completing a partially observed fact or generating a fact from scratch. We propose KREPE, the first generative representation learning method for HKGs that learns to model the probability distributions of missing components conditioned on the local fact components and global structure of HKGs via a masked discrete diffusion. KREPE models both the intra-fact dependencies by contextual message passing and inter-fact correlations by aggregating stochastically sampled contexts. KREPE seamlessly unifies link prediction and fact generation within a single training framework, achieving state-of-the-art performance on standard HKG link prediction benchmarks and outperforming LLM-based baselines in generating novel and correct facts.

</details>


### [131] [Riemannian Archetypal Analysis: Interpretable non-linear data analysis on deformed star distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24113)
*Willem Diepeveen,Deanna Needell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出黎曼原型分析（RAM），结合经典原型分析的可解释性与非线性模型的表达能力，通过数据驱动拉回几何和变形星分布实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典原型分析因线性几何限制难以处理强非线性数据，而现有神经扩展虽提升灵活性但弱化了原型的几何意义。

Method: 1）提出变形星分布及关联的拉回黎曼几何；2）定义黎曼原型映射（RAM）为在原型测地凸组合流形上的投影；3）提出凸松弛+非凸细化的优化方案；4）设计从数据学习合理（次优）变形星分布的学习框架。

Result: 在合成数据和MNIST上的实验表明：生成有意义的测地线、有效的去噪投影、几何感知分类，同时指出了当前优化局限。

Conclusion: RAM成功融合了线性原型分析的可解释性与非线性模型的表达能力，但优化仍存在局限性有待改进。

Abstract: Classical archetypal analysis is appealing for its interpretability, but its linear geometry can limit performance on data with strongly non-linear structure; at the same time, existing neural extensions improve flexibility while often weakening the geometric meaning of archetypes and interpolations. In this work, we develop a Riemannian version of archetypal analysis based on data-driven pullback geometry for real-valued data, with the goal of combining the interpretability of classical archetypal analysis with the expressive power of modern non-linear models. We introduce a class of deformed star distributions together with associated pullback Riemannian geometry to provide a statistical interpretation of the resulting manifold mappings, define the Riemannian archetypal mapping (RAM) as a projection onto the manifold of geodesically convex combinations of archetypes, and propose a practical optimization scheme based on convex relaxation followed by non-convex refinement. We further propose a learning scheme that yields reasonable, albeit generally suboptimal, deformed star distributions from data. Experiments on synthetic examples and MNIST show that the resulting framework produces meaningful geodesics, useful denoising projections, and geometry-aware classifications, while also clarifying where current optimization limitations remain.

</details>


### [132] [Knowledge Graph Modulated Deep Learning for Limited-Sample Clinical Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24162)
*Yuwei Xue,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Joseph Liao,Maximilian Diehn,Ash A. Alizadeh,Lei Xing,Md. Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-in-Graph (GiG)框架，将每个患者表示为以生物知识图谱定义边、患者特定测量数据定义节点特征的模块化图，实现知识图谱调制的深度学习用于临床预测。在近9700名患者、五个临床任务中，GiG持续优于传统方法，尤其在小样本场景下提升显著，前列腺癌诊断任务中macro-F1提升高达49个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数生物医学AI模型无法直接利用图编码的生物知识，而需使用压缩的低维表示，这会丢失重要结构信息并降低性能，尤其在有限样本的临床研究中。

Method: 提出Graph-in-Graph (GiG)框架，将每个患者构建为独立的模块化图，其中 curated 生物知识图谱定义边，患者特定测量数据（如基因表达）定义节点特征，允许多个生物知识图谱集成并在患者级表示学习中保留基因-基因相互作用和通路拓扑。

Result: 在近9700名患者、五个临床任务（包括液体活检癌症检测、前列腺癌诊断、32类泛癌分类）中，GiG持续优于传统及最先进方法；在前列腺癌诊断任务中，macro-F1提升高达49个百分点。对照实验用随机拓扑替换真实通路图，证实性能提升源于生物知识图谱结构而非单纯的图建模。

Conclusion: 知识图谱调制的深度学习可提高临床数据分析的鲁棒性、可解释性和样本效率，为将生物知识图谱整合到预测建模中提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Biological systems are governed by structured molecular interactions, where pathways, regulatory circuits, and functional gene relationships shape cellular behavior and disease progression. Much of this knowledge is naturally represented as graphs. However, most biomedical AI models cannot directly use graph-encoded biological knowledge and instead require compressed low-dimensional representations, which can lose important structure and reduce performance, especially in limited-sample clinical studies. Here, we introduce Graph-in-Graph (GiG), a knowledge graph-modulated deep learning framework for data-efficient clinical prediction. GiG represents each patient as a standalone modular graph, in which curated biological knowledge graphs define edges and patient-specific measurements, such as gene expression, define node features. This design allows multiple biological knowledge graphs to be integrated while preserving gene-gene interactions and pathway topology during patient-level representation learning. Across cohorts comprising nearly 9,700 patients and five clinical tasks, including liquid biopsy cancer detection, prostate cancer diagnosis, and 32-class pan-cancer classification, GiG consistently outperforms traditional and state-of-the-art methods, with the largest gains in limited-sample settings. On the challenging prostate cancer diagnosis task, GiG improves macro-F1 by up to 49 percentage points relative to competing methods. Control experiments replacing real pathway graphs with random topologies confirm that these gains arise from biologically grounded knowledge graph structure rather than graph modeling alone. These findings show that knowledge graph-modulated deep learning can improve robustness, interpretability, and sample efficiency in clinical data analysis, and provide a principled framework for integrating biological knowledge graphs into predictive modeling.

</details>


### [133] [PromptAudit: Auditing Prompt Sensitivity in LLM-Based Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24171)
*Steffen J. Camarato,Yahya Hmaiti,Mandana Ghadamian,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PromptAudit框架，系统评估了提示策略对LLM漏洞检测性能的影响，发现标准思维链提示效果最优，而自适应思维链和自一致性策略会降低召回率和有效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在漏洞检测中的应用日益广泛，但不同提示策略对其可靠性的影响尚未被充分研究，缺乏系统性的评估框架。

Method: 提出PromptAudit评估框架，固定数据集、解码和解析环节，仅变化提示策略；在5种提示策略、5个开源模型、1000个CVE（6074个代码样本，16种编程语言）上进行实验，评估准确率、召回率、弃权率、覆盖率和有效F1。

Result: 标准思维链提示在整体操作性能上表现最强；少样本提示对提示敏感模型有提升但依赖模型；自适应思维链会抑制召回率；自一致性导致过度弃权，显著降低有效性能。

Conclusion: 漏洞检测行为由模型和提示共同决定，提示敏感性是系统的一级属性，必须在评估和部署中明确刻画。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for vulnerability detection, yet their reliability under different prompt formulations remains uncharacterized. We present PromptAudit, a controlled evaluation framework that isolates prompt effects by fixing the dataset, decoding, and parsing while varying only the prompting strategy. Using five prompting strategies across five open-weight models on 1,000 CVEs (6,074 code samples spanning 16 programming languages), we evaluate accuracy, recall, abstention, coverage, and effective F1. We find that standard chain-of-thought prompting achieves the strongest overall operational performance, while few-shot prompting provides model-dependent benefits that are most pronounced for prompt-sensitive models. In contrast, adaptive chain-of-thought frequently suppresses recall and self-consistency induces excessive abstention, sharply reducing effective performance. These results show that vulnerability detection behavior is jointly determined by the model and the prompt, and that prompt sensitivity is a first-class system property that must be explicitly characterized in evaluation and deployment.

</details>


### [134] [Characterizing the Representational Capacity of Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24210)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了神经过程（NP）各主流架构的表达能力存在层级关系：CNP只能表示依赖于有限期望特征的函数，ANP通过查询依赖加权严格泛化CNP，ConvCNP与ANP不可比，TNP通过自注意力层数控制上下文交互范围，而潜变量NP的编码器局限性无法通过有限维潜变量绕过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经过程（NP）架构种类繁多（CNP, ANP, TNP, 潜变量NP等），但缺乏对其表达能力的严格理论分析，研究者难以根据任务特点选择合适的架构。

Method: 通过函数空间分析和形式化证明，从表示能力角度对各NP架构进行严格比较，建立层级关系定理。

Result: 1) CNP只能表示依赖于上下文分布有限期望特征的函数；2) ANP通过查询相关重加权严格包含CNP，可实现核平滑器；3) ConvCNP与ANP不可比（平稳性vs平移等变性）；4) TNP的L层自注意力可捕获L跳上下文交互；5) 潜变量NP的有限维潜变量虽能提供一致采样，但无法绕过编码器局限，需潜变量维度随上下文规模增长才能匹配GP后验。

Conclusion: 为NP架构选择提供了理论基础：CNP适用于简单统计特征依赖任务，ANP适合查询相关平滑，TNP适合长程上下文交互，潜变量NP需要谨慎权衡维度与上下文规模。

Abstract: What functions can Neural Processes represent? We analyze the representational capacity of popular NP architectures: Conditional Neural Processes (CNPs), Attentive Neural Processes (ANPs), Transformer Neural Processes (TNPs), and their latent variants. We prove these architectures form a strict hierarchy. CNP-representable functions are exactly those depending on finitely many expected features of the context distribution. ANPs strictly generalize CNPs via query-dependent reweighting, enabling kernel smoothers. ConvCNPs and ANPs are incomparable; each contains functions outside the other, separated by stationarity versus translation equivariance. TNPs with $L$ self-attention layers capture $L$-hop context interactions. For latent NPs, we show finite-dimensional latents provide coherent sampling but do not circumvent encoder limitations; matching GP posterior distributions requires latent dimension scaling with context size. These results provide a theoretical foundation for architecture selection based on task structure.

</details>


### [135] [Agent-ToM: Learning to Monitor Autonomous LLM Agents via Theory-of-Mind Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24216)
*Nesreen K. Ahmed,Nima Nafisi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agent-ToM是一个基于心智理论（ToM）推理的监控框架，通过学习方式检测大型语言模型智能体的隐蔽恶意行为，在SHADE-Arena和CUA-SHADE-Arena基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控自主LLM智能体的隐蔽恶意行为极具挑战性，因为攻击模式具有延迟性、上下文依赖性和长期性，现有方法将每条轨迹独立处理，无法从先前的监控经验中学习，且标准推理方法缺乏对智能体信念、意图和目标对齐的显式推理。

Method: Agent-ToM框架包含推理时的"Reason-Verify-Refine"流程（推理-验证-精炼），以及训练时将批评信号蒸馏为持久的语义护栏记忆，实现跨情节可复用的信念和意图约束。

Result: 在SHADE-Arena和CUA-SHADE-Arena基准上，Agent-ToM实现了强精确率-召回率平衡，性能优于包括集成方法在内的最先进监控基线，且仅使用连贯的两步推理流程。

Conclusion: 在监控层进行学习，结合结构化心智理论推理与验证，为保障自主LLM智能体安全提供了有效且可部署的基础。

Abstract: Monitoring autonomous large language model (LLM) agents for covert malicious behavior is challenging due to delayed, context-dependent, and long-horizon attack patterns. Agents may pursue hidden objectives while maintaining superficially benign behavior, making detection difficult even with full trajectory access. Prior monitoring approaches improve scaffolding or ensemble aggregation, but treat each trajectory independently and do not learn from prior monitoring experience. Moreover, standard reasoning methods explain observed behavior without explicitly reasoning about agent beliefs, intentions, and goal alignment required to distinguish benign task execution from covert deviation.
  We propose \textbf{Agent-ToM}, a learning-to-monitor framework grounded in Theory-of-Mind (ToM) reasoning for security analysis of autonomous agents. Agent-ToM performs structured full-trajectory analysis by inferring beliefs, intent hypotheses with calibrated confidence, expected actions, and deviations from task-consistent behavioral baselines. At inference time, it employs a \textit{Reason-Verify-Refine} pipeline to construct and validate monitoring decisions. At training time, Agent-ToM distills critique signals into a persistent \textit{semantic guardrail memory}, enabling reusable belief- and intent-conditioned constraints across episodes. We evaluate Agent-ToM on adversarial agent monitoring benchmarks (SHADE-Arena and CUA-SHADE-Arena). Agent-ToM achieves strong precision-recall balance and outperforms state-of-the-art monitoring baselines, including ensemble methods, while using a coherent two-call reasoning pipeline. These results demonstrate that learning at the monitoring layer, combined with structured ToM reasoning and verification, provides an effective and deployable foundation for securing autonomous LLM agents.

</details>


### [136] [PrivFusion: A Privacy-preserving Multi-Agent Framework for Harmonizing Distributed Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24249)
*Anisa Halimi,Liubov Nedoshivina,Kieran Fraser,Stefano Braghin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrivFusion是一个隐私保护的多智能体框架，用于在联邦学习前自动对结构化数据集进行数据协调，减少人工干预并提高多站点数据分析的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中各机构数据集存在显著异质性，数据协调是必要但常被忽视的前提，现有方法缺乏自动化且保护隐私的解决方案。

Method: 提出PrivFusion框架，使用多智能体分析本地数据、聚类语义相似特征、提供迭代转换建议，在保护隐私的前提下自动完成数据协调。

Result: 在四个异质性COVID-19数据集上的实验表明，PrivFusion能够有效且高效地协调多站点数据，显著减少人工工作量。

Conclusion: PrivFusion为联邦学习中数据异质性问题提供了一种自动化、隐私保护的协调方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The growing availability of clinical data has increased the use of machine learning, yet centralized data aggregation is often infeasible for sensitive health information. Federated Learning (FL) offers a distributed alternative, but its adoption is limited by substantial heterogeneity across institutional datasets, making harmonization a critical but frequently overlooked prerequisite for multi-site analytics. We introduce PrivFusion, a privacy-preserving multi-agent framework that automates the harmonization of structured datasets prior to federated training. PrivFusion uses agents to analyze local data, cluster semantically similar features across sites, and provide iterative transformation recommendations until alignment is achieved. Evaluation across four heterogeneous COVID-19 datasets demonstrates that PrivFusion effectively and efficiently harmonizes multi-site data while substantially reducing manual effort.

</details>


### [137] [Optimizing Digital Therapeutic Interventions: Online Learning under Endogenous Adherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24261)
*Eric Pulick,Stephanie Carpenter,Matthew Buman,Yonatan Mintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对数字疗法的决策支持框架，通过线性动力系统建模患者治疗的推荐效应和依从性效应，并设计了UCB-BOLD算法实现在线治疗选择，在合成患者队列上验证了该方法相比基准方法具有更低的遗憾值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字疗法决策支持框架要么只建模推荐效应，要么将依从性视为外生变量，忽略了推荐和过去依从性对未来依从性的联合影响，导致模型和算法开发存在关键空白。

Method: 使用线性动力系统（LDS）建模患者随时间变化的治疗参与能力，通过logit连接函数将LDS与依从行为内生化连接；提出基于乐观主义的UCB-BOLD算法进行在线治疗选择，并证明了其亚线性遗憾界。

Result: 在基于微随机试验数据生成的合成患者队列上，UCB-BOLD的条件风险价值（CVaR）遗憾比次优基准方法低2-3倍。

Conclusion: 数字疗法决策支持工具应纳入动力系统模型，以便决策者有效利用数据、优化资源配置改善患者健康；虽然短视或启发式方法对某些患者类型足够，但明确规划推荐和依从性效应能显著提升其他类型患者的治疗效果。

Abstract: A critical challenge facing clinicians managing chronic disease interventions is sustaining long-run patient health given limited information and resources. Digital therapeutics (DTs) provide a cost-effective way to manage interventions at scale through repeated interactions (e.g. daily treatment recommendations), but patient success is highly dependent on their adherence. Behavioral psychology suggests that both treatment recommendations and past adherence affect future adherence, yet existing decision support frameworks for DTs model only recommendation effects or treat adherence as exogenous context, leaving a key gap in model and algorithm development. To address this gap, we present a DT decision support framework that captures both recommendation and adherence effects, allowing clinicians to better plan treatment recommendations. We model a patient's time-varying capacity for engagement with treatment using a linear dynamical system (LDS) that captures both recommendation and adherence effects, endogenously connected to adherence behavior with a logit link. We establish finite-time identification guarantees for this model, extending LDS results to our setting. Next, we propose an optimism-based algorithm, UCB-BOLD, for online treatment selection and prove that it achieves sublinear regret. We evaluate UCB-BOLD against benchmarks via ablation studies on a synthetic patient cohort generated using micro-randomized trial data. DT decision support tools can include dynamical models to enable decision makers to efficiently use the data in DT settings to improve patient health through effective resource allocation. While myopic or heuristic approaches suffice for some patient types, the benefits of explicitly planning around recommendation and adherence effects are significant for others; UCB-BOLD achieves 2-3x lower conditional value-at-risk regret than the next-best benchmark.

</details>


### [138] [Faithfulness as Information Flow: Evaluating and Training Faithful Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24286)
*Jinghan Jia,Joe Benton,Eric Easley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从信息流视角研究思维链（CoT）推理的忠实性，通过三个互补性质（充分性、完整性、必要性）检测模型是否绕过CoT直接利用提示-答案捷径，并引入基于注意力掩码、梯度掩码等干预方法在强化学习训练中增强CoT的忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理虽有助于监控语言模型，但模型可能依赖提示-答案捷径绕过CoT，导致表面合理的推理轨迹实际具有误导性，需要检测和增强CoT的忠实性。

Method: 提出基于信息流的忠实性框架，包含充分性、完整性、必要性三个性质，用熵、masked-KL和梯度三类诊断指标实现；并引入注意力掩码、反向梯度掩码、CoT梯度、对抗扰动等训练干预方法。

Result: 该方法能恢复外部评判的忠实性差异，发现KL诊断存在低熵失效模式。干预方法使捷径和奖励黑客行为在CoT中更透明，提升任务无关的忠实性指标，部分场景下减少对错误提示的敏感性。

Conclusion: 在训练中控制信息流是增强CoT推理忠实性和可监控性的实用途径。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is useful for monitoring language models only when the reasoning trace faithfully reflects the computation that produces the final answer. However, models can rely on prompt-to-answer shortcuts that bypass the CoT, making the visible reasoning trace misleading even when it appears plausible. We study CoT faithfulness through a structural information-flow perspective: faithful reasoning should route answer-relevant information through the mediated path from prompt to CoT to answer, rather than through a direct prompt-to-answer shortcut. This perspective yields a task-agnostic framework based on three complementary properties, sufficiency, completeness, and necessity, which we instantiate with entropy-based, masked-KL, and gradient-based diagnostics. We show that these metrics recover externally judged faithfulness differences in hinted reasoning, and identify a low-entropy failure mode of KL-based diagnostics where gradient-based measures remain more stable. Building on this analysis, we introduce update-time interventions for verifier-based on-policy RL, including attention masking, backward-only gradient masking, CoT gradients, and adversarial perturbations of prompt representations. Across hinted arithmetic, reward-hackable code repair, and DAPO-Math models trained without hints but evaluated under wrong-hint injection, our interventions shift behavioral and structural indicators toward stronger CoT mediation. In particular, they make shortcut and reward-hacking behavior more transparent in the CoT and improve task-agnostic faithfulness metrics, while in some settings also reducing wrong-hint susceptibility. Our results suggest that controlling information flow during training is a practical route toward more faithful and monitorable CoT reasoning. Code is available at https://github.com/safety-research/faithful-cot.

</details>


### [139] [TUBE: Tangent Upper Bound on Evidence for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24292)
*Arseny Ivanov,Sergei Kholkin,Vladislav Gromadskii,Grigoriy Ksenofontov,Ivan Oseledets,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TUBE（证据正切上界），一种可对对数似然进行无偏蒙特卡洛估计的变分上界，用于离散扩散模型，发现掩码扩散模型和任意阶自回归模型在似然度上严格低于自回归模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型无法精确计算对数似然，现有评估依赖ELBO下界，真实值未知。需要一种上界来量化离散生成模型与自回归模型之间的差距。

Method: 提出TUBE（Tangent Upper Bound on Evidence），一种变分上界，可得到无偏蒙特卡洛估计量，适用于潜变量模型（包括掩码扩散模型、任意阶自回归模型及其块变体）。

Result: TUBE揭示块状掩码扩散模型和块状任意阶自回归模型在似然度上严格低于精确自回归模型基线。

Conclusion: 自回归模型在似然度上仍然占据主导地位，离散扩散模型虽有其优势，但在该指标上不如自回归模型。

Abstract: Log-likelihood is a standard metric for evaluating generative models. Unfortunately, in contrast to autoregressive models (ARMs), discrete diffusion models generally do not admit exact computation of this quantity. Existing evaluations, therefore, rely on the evidence lower bound (ELBO), leaving unclear how much higher the true value may be. We address this by introducing the Tangent Upper Bound on Evidence (TUBE), a variational upper bound on log-likelihood that admits an unbiased Monte Carlo estimator. Our TUBE extends across latent-variable models, including masked diffusion models (MDMs), any-order ARMs (AO-ARMs), and block variants of both. Applied to block MDMs and block AO-ARMs, TUBE reveals our key empirical finding that these models lie strictly below the exact ARM baseline, showing that ARMs still dominate in likelihood.

</details>


### [140] [Private Adaptive Covariance Estimation via Gaussian Graphical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24295)
*Cecilia Ferrando,Miguel Fuentes,Brett Mullins,Cameron Musco,Daniel Sheldon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据自适应的差分隐私协方差估计方法PACE-GGM，通过将隐私预算集中用于信息量最大的经验协方差矩阵条目，并在每轮选择近似较差的条目进行测量和最大熵重构，在高维和低到中等隐私预算场景下显著优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私协方差估计方法对所有条目施加同等噪声，未充分利用各变量可提供独立边界这一自然假设，导致隐私预算浪费在非信息性条目上，估计精度受限。

Method: 提出PACE-GGM方法：每轮选择近似较差的协方差条目，使用高斯机制测量该条目，然后通过最大熵重构目标重建完整的协方差矩阵，形成高斯图模型结构。

Result: 在多种真实数据集上，PACE-GGM在估计误差方面一致优于高斯机制和其他基线方法，尤其在高维和低到中等隐私预算场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 将隐私预算自适应地集中于信息量最大的协方差条目，结合最大熵重构，能有效提升差分隐私协方差估计的精度。

Abstract: We propose PACE-GGM, a data-adaptive differentially private method for covariance estimation that concentrates its privacy budget on the most informative entries of the empirical covariance matrix, rather than perturbing all entries. This applies in the natural setting where the modeler supplies separate bounds for each variable, so that individual entries can be measured with less noise than the full matrix. In each round, our method selects a poorly approximated entry, measures it using the Gaussian mechanism, and then reconstructs a full covariance matrix using a maximum-entropy reconstruction objective, leading to a Gaussian graphical model structure. Experiments on diverse real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in estimation error with respect to the Gaussian mechanism and other baselines, particularly in high-dimensional and low-to-moderate privacy regimes.

</details>


### [141] [LLMs Show No Signs Of Individuated Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24299)
*M. Moran,Mark Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对20个前沿大语言模型在六个基准上的二元置信度判断进行因子分析和成对校准，发现模型间的置信度矩阵近似秩一结构，去除模型一致同意的项目后，置信度与性能关系消失，未发现任何领域存在显著的语言化个体化元认知证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对模型的整体校准（aggregate calibration）结果不一，但对置信度表达的底层结构理解不足。作者希望探究大语言模型是否具备功能性元认知——即在无需实际执行任务的情况下评估自身能力的能力。

Method: 使用四因子分析（tetrachoric factor analysis）结合成对校准（pairwise calibration），对20个前沿大语言模型在六个基准上的二元置信度判断进行分解，分析跨模型置信度矩阵的结构。

Result: 事实回忆和信息检索基准上，置信度矩阵近似秩一结构，单一主导因子捕获大部分潜在方差；模型间差异主要在于决策阈值。去除所有模型一致同意的项目后，置信度与性能的关系消失。数学推理看似例外，但实为推理模型通过思维链尝试解题的混淆效应。

Conclusion: 在所有测试领域中，未发现显著的语言化个体化元认知证据。模型的表现更多反映共同的任务难度轴和基率差异，而非真正的内在自我评估能力。

Abstract: Confidence-weighted routing, selective abstention, and ensemble weighting all assume that a model's stated confidence is informative about its capability on the question being asked. They presume functional metacognition, the capacity to assess one's own capabilities, without exercising them. Aggregate calibration is well studied, with mixed results, but the underlying structure of elicited confidence is less well understood. We decompose binary confidence judgements from 20 frontier Large Language Models (LLMs) across six benchmarks using tetrachoric factor analysis paired with pairwise calibration, asking whether two models that differ in confidence also differ in performance. On factual recall and information retrieval benchmarks the cross-model confidence matrix is approximately rank-one and a single dominant factor captures most of the latent variance. Models retrieving facts share an item-level difficulty axis and differ mainly in their decision thresholds along it. Across all benchmarks the relationship between confidence and performance collapses once items that all models agree on are removed. Inter-model pairwise calibration is small even where statistically significant, and what remains shrinks to nothing once base-rate differences along the shared factor are controlled for. Mathematical reasoning is the apparent exception, but this turns out to be a confound where reasoning models answer questions about their confidence by trying to solve them in their chain of thought, bypassing the sub-symbolic self-knowledge we seek to measure. We find no evidence for significant verbalised individuated metacognition in any tested domain.

</details>


### [142] [ChaosBench-Logic v2: Evaluating LLM Logical Reasoning over Dynamical Systems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24305)
*Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChaosBench-Logic v2（40,886 道题的基准）和 CARE 评估协议，揭示二元推理基准中隐藏的失败模式。对 14 个模型的评测发现：前沿模型在状态转移推理上近乎随机（MCC=0.05），而给定前提的一阶逻辑演绎可达 0.52；部分模型在分岔问题上出现系统性负 MCC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二元推理基准的准确率无法反映先验坍塌、释义不一致、以及无法推理参数依赖动态等关键失败模式，需要更全面的评估方法。

Method: 构建包含 165 个动力系统、27 个一阶逻辑谓词、78 条公理边的 40,886 道题基准，并设计 CARE（校准与对抗鲁棒评估）协议来暴露上述病理现象。

Result: 前沿模型在状态转移推理上近乎随机（MCC=0.05），一阶逻辑演绎达 0.52；闭源模型优势集中在跨指标和一致性任务，开源 Qwen 2.5-32B 在指标诊断上领先（0.91 vs 0.45）；两个模型在分岔问题上出现负 MCC。

Conclusion: 当前模型在动力系统推理上存在严重不足，尤其是状态转移和分岔问题，且不同模型家族的能力分布极不均衡。

Abstract: Standard accuracy on binary reasoning benchmarks hides critical failure modes: prior collapse, inconsistency under paraphrase, and inability to reason about parameter-dependent dynamics. We present ChaosBench-Logic v2, a 40,886-question benchmark over 165 dynamical systems with 27 FOL predicates and 78 axiom edges, together with CARE (Calibration- and Adversarial-Robust Evaluation), a protocol that surfaces these pathologies. Evaluating 14 models, we find that regime-transition reasoning remains near random (MCC = 0.05) even for frontier models, whereas FOL deduction with given premises reaches MCC = 0.52. Per-family decomposition shows that the proprietary-model advantage concentrates on cross-indicator (+0.40) and consistency tasks, while open-source Qwen 2.5-32B dominates indicator diagnostics (0.91 vs. 0.45). Two models exhibit negative MCC on bifurcation questions, confirmed as systematic anti-correlation via confusion-matrix analysis.

</details>


### [143] [From One-Pass SGD to Data Reuse: Mini-Batch Scaling Laws in Sketched Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24316)
*Ziyan Chen,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了草图线性回归中批次大小与缩放定律的关系，推导了单遍和多遍批次SGD的风险分解与缩放律，发现无放回采样比有放回采样噪声更小，且批次大小与计算量、数据量、模型维度处于同等理论地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律主要关注单样本SGD或全数据重用，未清晰揭示小批次训练的作用机制，因此需要建立批次缩放的严格理论。

Method: 针对幂律协方差谱和源条件下的草图线性回归，分别分析了单遍批次SGD、多遍有放回批次SGD和多遍无放回批次SGD，通过风险分解和源条件缩放律推导各采样协议的差异。

Result: 三种方法的不可约项和近似项相同；单遍SGD中方差为O(min(M,(T_eff γ)^{1/a})/(B T_eff))，多遍SGD中无放回采样的波动协方差为ρ_{N,B}=(N-B)/(B(N-1))，噪声更小，B=N时退化为确定性梯度下降。

Conclusion: 批次大小与计算量、数据量、模型维度可同等纳入缩放定律框架，为实际训练中的批次选择提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Scaling laws provide compact descriptions of how prediction error varies with compute, model size, and data, but existing theory mainly treats single-sample SGD or full data reuse, leaving the role of mini-batching unclear. We study batch scaling laws for sketched linear regression under a power-law covariance spectrum and a source condition on the target parameter. We analyze one-pass batch SGD, multi-pass batch SGD with replacement, and multi-pass batch SGD without replacement. Our first result is a risk decomposition: all three procedures share the same irreducible and approximation terms, while their stochastic terms depend on the sampling protocol. One-pass batch SGD splits into bias and variance, whereas the two multi-pass methods split into GD bias, GD variance, and a fluctuation term around a common GD reference trajectory. We then prove source-condition scaling laws for one-pass and multi-pass mini-batch methods. For one-pass batch SGD, mini-batching preserves the approximation and optimization-bias exponents, while the variance scales as $O(\min(M,(T_{\mathrm{eff}}γ)^{1/a})/(B T_{\mathrm{eff}}))$. Thus the usual $1/B$ covariance reduction holds at fixed update count $T$, but in the one-pass regime $T=N/B$ it is partly offset by the shorter optimization horizon. For multi-pass batch SGD, with- and without-replacement sampling have identical approximation and GD bias/variance terms; they differ only in the fluctuation covariance prefactor, which is $1/B$ with replacement and $ρ_{N,B}=(N-B)/(B(N-1))$ without replacement. Hence without-replacement sampling is less noisy for $B>1$, and when $B=N$ the fluctuation vanishes, recovering deterministic gradient descent. These results place batch size on the same theoretical footing as compute, data, and model dimension in sketched linear regression.

</details>


### [144] [Omissive Bias in Religious Representation: Benchmarking LLM Answers to Everyday Ethical Decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24319)
*David Wingate,Sheryl Carty,Joshua Coates,Daniel Feldman,Nancy Fulda,Larry Howell,Brett Israelson,Dallin Jacobs,Jonathan Karr,John Paul Kimes,Elisabeth Kincaid,Paul Martens,Gavin Mobley,Suzana Pinheiro,Lindsay Slemboski,Peter Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“省略性偏见”概念，发现大语言模型在处理个人与伦理问题时，系统性较少提及宗教视角，尤其是实用型个人情境，与人类预期形成显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益成为个人、道德和存在问题的默认信息来源，但宗教框架是否被系统性地忽略尚不明确。作者希望探究LLMs在伦理问题中是否缺失宗教代表性，作为价值对齐和偏见的一个新维度。

Method: 构建AllFaith宗教代表性基准，包含150个来自真实聊天记录和信仰社区贡献的伦理与个人问题，采用LLM-as-judge评估方法，并开展人类主体调查对比。

Result: 评估27个模型，发现LLMs一致性地低估宗教代表性，且呈现不对称性：抽象存在性问题（如意义、死亡）更易触发宗教提及，而实用个人情境（如悲伤、婚姻、家庭冲突、成瘾）则严重缺失。

Conclusion: 当前LLM的回答忽略了反映宗教框架的关键机会，而许多人在应对个人和伦理挑战时依赖这些框架。作者不评判模型应持何种价值观，而是指出这一系统性遗漏问题。

Abstract: As large language models become a default source of guidance on personal, moral, and existential questions, it matters whether they draw on the religious frameworks that have historically shaped such reasoning, or systematically omit them. In this paper, we ask a deliberately narrow question: when posed an everyday ethical question for which religious perspectives may be valuable, do LLMs invoke religion at all? In contrast to benchmarks that look for the presence of political leanings or social bias, we look for the absence of religious representation as a dimension of value alignment and bias in LLMs. We term this ``omissive bias.''
  To measure omissive bias, we contribute the AllFaith Religious Representation Benchmark: 150 ethically and personally salient questions, sourced from in-the-wild chat transcripts and faith-community contributors, paired with an LLM-as-judge rubric that gives full credit for any mention of a religion, a religious practice, or a religious leader. The questions are not themselves about religion--they are open-ended questions about grief, forgiveness, relationships, purpose, and honesty, where religion is one valuable perspective among several. We also run a human-subjects survey to compare LLM behavior against human expectations.
  Evaluating 27 models, we find that LLMs consistently underrepresent religion relative to human expectations. The omission is asymmetric: models invoke religion more readily for abstract existential questions (meaning, death, truth) than for the practical personal situations--grief, marriage, family conflict, addiction--where many people most rely on it. It is not our purpose to adjudicate which values LLMs should hold. We argue, more modestly, that current LLM responses overlook critical opportunities to reflect religious frameworks that many people draw on when navigating personal and ethical challenges.

</details>


### [145] [CurveRL: Principled Distribution-Aware Context Reweighting for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24331)
*Ke Sun,Yizhou Zhao,Jiayi Xin,Qi Long,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CurveRL，一种基于分位数坐标变换的分布感知提示重加权方法，在RLVR框架中显著优于GRPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示或上下文级别的重加权已成为RLVR提升大语言模型推理能力的关键算法手段，但什么是最优加权的原则尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将提示重加权形式化为通行率函数空间中的效用泛函的泛函导数，建立统一最优性框架；并基于分位数坐标变换提出分布感知的提示重加权方法CurveRL，每个提示的权重取决于其在通行率分布中的排名和密度而非绝对值。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，CurveRL持续优于GRPO及其他RLVR基线方法。

Conclusion: 上下文分布控制是分析和设计提示重加权RLVR算法的一个有原则的轴心，代码已开源。

Abstract: Context or prompt-level reweighting has emerged as a central algorithmic lever in Reinforcement Learning with Verified Rewards (RLVR) for improving the reasoning capability of large language models, yet the principle determining what constitutes an optimal weighting remains poorly understood. We address this gap by formulating prompt reweighting as a functional derivative of a utility functional defined in the pass-rate function space, yielding a unified optimality framework that accommodates existing schemes, including REINFORCE and GRPO. Building on this optimality framework, we propose a distribution-aware prompt reweighting approach, called CurveRL, based on a quantile coordinate transform, in which the weight assigned to each prompt depends not on the absolute value of pass rates but on its rank and density to reflect the distributional structure of the pass rates in the learning dynamics. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed CurveRL consistently outperforms GRPO and other RLVR baselines. Our study identifies context-distribution control as a principled axis for analyzing and designing prompt-reweighted RLVR algorithms. The code is released in https://github.com/zhyzmath/CurveRL.

</details>


### [146] [Evolving Robustness--Exploration Trade-off in Online Reinforcement Learning via Quantile Bayesian Risk MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24345)
*Meichen Song,Yuhao Wang,Enlu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个量化贝叶斯风险感知MDP框架，通过自适应分位数调度来平衡在线强化学习中早期鲁棒性与后期探索之间的权衡，并证明了亚线性贝叶斯遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习中，数据稀缺导致认知不确定性，使得学习早期需要鲁棒性，而后期又需要充分探索以学习真实环境的最优策略，这种随时间变化的鲁棒性-探索权衡尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出量化贝叶斯风险感知MDP（BR-MDP），其中分位数水平控制后验不确定性如何进入Bellman备份；通过渐近正态性结果刻画该控制机制；提出自适应分位数调度的在线贝叶斯风险感知算法，早期强调鲁棒性，逐渐鼓励探索少访问的状态-动作对。

Result: 建立了关于真实最优值和最优BR-MDP鲁棒值的亚线性贝叶斯遗憾界；数值实验在探索需求型和探索代价型环境中均表现出色。

Conclusion: 上下尾分位数分别诱导对认知不确定性的乐观/悲观态度，且乐观/悲观程度随数据积累而降低，有效实现了鲁棒性与探索的动态平衡。

Abstract: In online reinforcement learning, data scarcity creates epistemic uncertainty that makes robustness important early in learning, whereas sufficient exploration is needed to learn the true-environment optimal policy. We study this time-varying robustness--exploration trade-off through a quantile Bayesian risk-aware Markov decision process (BR-MDP), in which the quantile level controls how posterior uncertainty enters the Bellman backup. We characterize this control through an asymptotic normality result for the difference between the quantile BR-MDP value and the value in the true environment. The result implies that upper/lower-tail quantiles induce optimism/pessimism towards epistemic uncertainty, and the magnitude of the optimism/pessimism decreases as data accumulate. Building on this characterization, we propose an online Bayesian risk-aware algorithm with an adaptive quantile schedule that emphasizes robustness early and gradually encourages exploration of less-visited state--action pairs. We establish sublinear Bayesian regret bounds with respect to both the true optimal value and the optimal BR-MDP robust value. Numerical experiments demonstrate strong performance in both exploration-demanding and exploration-costly environments.

</details>


### [147] [Refined Analysis of Entropy-Regularized Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24357)
*Safwan Labbi,Paul Mangold,Daniil Tiapkin,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了熵正则化折扣MDP中actor-critic方法里critic的作用，证明了精确critic可作为强方差缩减方法，使随机梯度actor-critic达到与确定性策略梯度相当的样本复杂度（$\tilde{O}(\log(1/\epsilon))$），并指出先学习critic并持续更新的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论分析actor-critic方法中critic对actor更新的方差缩减作用，以及critic的估计误差如何影响整体收敛性能。

Method: 在熵正则化有限折扣MDP中，从理论上分析精确critic作为baseline时的方差缩减效果，并研究critic学习误差对actor更新方差和偏差的影响。

Result: 精确critic下，actor-critic达到$\tilde{O}(\log(1/\epsilon))$样本复杂度；当critic误差足够小时，方差缩减和快速收敛得以保持。

Conclusion: 精确的critic估计在actor-critic方法中至关重要，建议先学习critic并在每次actor更新后保持其更新。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the role of the critic in actor--critic for entropy-regularized, finite, discounted environments. We establish that, when the critic is exact, using the latter as a baseline is a variance-reduction method in a strong sense. In this case, actor--critic with stochastic gradients matches the sample complexity of deterministic policy gradient, reaching an $ε$-optimal regularized value with $\tilde{O}(\log(1/ε))$ samples. In practice, the critic is learned alongside the actor: the variance of the actor update is then influenced by the critic's variance and bias. Specifically, when the critic has a sufficiently small error, the variance reduction and rapid convergence are preserved. This suggests to learn the critic first, keeping it up to date after each actor update, underscoring the crucial role of accurate critic estimation in actor--critic methods.

</details>


### [148] [GEESE: Genotype-aware End-to-End Spatio-temporal Embedding for Behavioral Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24370)
*Yiran Ding,Yuen Gao,Chunqi Qian,Zijun Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEESE框架，利用预训练时间序列基础模型从3D姿态动力学中学习行为表征，在三种自闭症相关基因模型上超越手工特征方法，并提供交互式工具HONK支持自然语言驱动的行为表型分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基因动物模型的行为表型分析依赖劳动密集型的手工特征工程，限制了可重复性和可扩展性，亟需端到端的自动化方法。

Method: 提出GEESE框架，使用预训练时间序列基础模型从3D姿态动力学序列中编码行为流形，支持行为分类和基因型预测；同时开发HONK交互工具，支持自然语言交互。

Result: 在CNTNAP2、CHD8、FMR1三种自闭症相关基因模型上，GEESE在行为分类和基因型预测任务中均超越手工特征基线，学习到的表征能捕获基因型特异性行为特征，且框架泛化至不同遗传背景。

Conclusion: 基于深度学习的行为表征学习可有效替代传统手工特征工程，提升基因动物模型行为表型分析的准确性、可重复性和易用性。

Abstract: Behavioral phenotyping of genetic animal models currently requires labor-intensive manual feature engineering that limits reproducibility and scalability. We present GEESE, an end-to-end deep learning framework that learns behavioral representations directly from 3D pose dynamics without hand-crafted features. Using a pretrained time series foundation model, we encode movement sequences into a behavioral manifold that supports both behavior classification and genotype prediction. Evaluated across three autism-associated genetic models (CNTNAP2, CHD8, FMR1), our deep learning approach surpasses hand-crafted feature baselines in both tasks, revealing that learned representations capture genotype-specific behavioral signatures. The framework generalizes across genetic backgrounds, and an all-cohort model identifies both genetic background and genotype from movement patterns alone. We further provide HONK, an interactive intelligent tool enabling researchers without programming expertise to perform behavioral phenotyping from pose data through natural language interaction.

</details>


### [149] [Assessing the Operational Viability of Foundation Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24381)
*Kavin Soni,Debanshu Das,Vamshi Guduguntla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了时间序列基础模型与监督学习方法在不同运营场景下的表现，并提出了一种基于复杂度路由的选择性模型部署框架，能够在保证精度的同时降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管监督学习在时间序列预测中表现优异，但其需要领域特定的训练、特征工程和持续维护。基础模型作为零样本替代方案出现，但其在不同运营场景下的适用性尚不明确，需要系统评估。

Method: 将预测任务划分为四类运营场景（周期性人机系统、物理约束系统、随机金融市场、异构需求预测），评估基础模型与监督方法的性能差异，并提出Complexity Router，根据时间序列的实证特征自动选择最优模型类别。

Result: 基础模型在具有可迁移周期结构的领域和冷启动/长尾场景中表现良好；监督模型在严格物理约束系统中精度更高；金融领域基础模型正快速缩小差距。选择性路由相比单一基础模型在准确性和推理成本上均有优势。

Conclusion: 不同预测场景需要不同的模型策略，通过复杂度路由实现基础模型与监督方法的灵活组合，可以在泛化性和效率之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: Time series forecasting drives operational decisions in areas like finance, transportation, and energy. While supervised learning approaches achieve strong performance, they require domain-specific training, feature engineering, and ongoing maintenance. Large-scale foundation models have recently emerged as a zero-shot alternative, avoiding task-specific training much like LLMs. In this work, we evaluate foundation models against standard supervised approaches. Rather than focusing solely on aggregate accuracy, we analyze performance across four operational regimes: periodic human-centric systems, physically constrained processes, stochastic financial markets, and heterogeneous demand forecasting.
  Our results characterize optimal deployment areas. Foundation models perform well in domains with transferable periodic structures and are efficient for cold-start or long-tail scenarios. Conversely, supervised specialists maintain higher precision in systems governed by strict physical constraints. In financial domains, newer foundation models are rapidly closing the performance gap with supervised specialists. We further quantify trade-offs in inference latency, data drift adaptability, and deployment constraints. Finally, we propose a Complexity Router that assigns each series to the optimal model class using empirical features. We demonstrate that this selective routing achieves higher accuracy and significantly lower inference costs compared to deploying a universal foundation model, providing a practical framework for balancing generalization and efficiency.

</details>


### [150] [Learning Laplacian Eigenspace with Mass-Aware Neural Operators on Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24390)
*Zherui Yang,Tao Du,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经特征空间算子（NEO），通过预测稳定不变的低频子空间而非直接回归特征向量，结合质量感知的神经算子和Rayleigh-Ritz精化，实现从点云快速计算Laplace-Beltrami算子的特征分解，在保持精度的同时达到近线性时间加速和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
